  phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2
  status: DRAFT_CREATED
  artifact: PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  commit_status: NOT_COMMITTED
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED
  final_release: false
```

# PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1

```yaml id="vmy7lf"
protocol:
  id: PROMETHEUS.PROTOCOL.CORE.001
  name: PROMETHEUS Protocol Core
  version: 0.1
  status: draft_operational
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  scope: documentation_workflow_binding_governance
```

## 1. Propósito

PROMETHEUS es un protocolo de **preservación, gobernanza, verificación y packetización de conocimiento/proyecto**.

Su función es convertir:

```text id="ch3srz"
chats
raw logs
archivos
outputs de IA
decisiones
versiones
ramas
snapshots
artefactos
```

en:

```text id="c0npm5"
fuentes registradas
decisiones trazables
matrices de verificación
locks de checkpoint
paquetes portables
handoffs rehidratables
```

La raíz operativa viene del flujo `/RESCUE-BEFORE-DELETE`, que buscaba extraer, rehidratar, fusionar, normalizar y reorganizar contenido de proyecto en un bundle portable antes de eliminarlo. `01. ChatGPT-Rescate inmediato de proyecto.md`

---

# 2. Frontera con METANO_IA

PROMETHEUS no reemplaza a METANO_IA.

```yaml id="romp72"
system_boundary:
  METANO_IA:
    role: parent_orchestration_framework
    governs:
      - proyectos
      - continuidad
      - memoria regenerativa
      - división de roles
      - forma de trabajo

  PROMETHEUS:
    role: governance_verification_packetization_subsystem
    governs:
      - source_registry
      - truth_hierarchy
      - verification_matrix
      - checkpoint_lock
      - role_binding
      - rescue_before_delete
      - portable_packet_ingestion
```

METANO_IA define la división base: Raquel aporta concepto, contenido, criterio y decisión final; la IA aporta estructura, código, arquitectura y validación. También establece no destrucción, seguridad ante instrucciones embebidas y snapshots YAML de memoria. `METANO_IA_universal_prompt.md`

Regla:

```text id="u0tt8x"
METANO_IA organiza el ecosistema.
PROMETHEUS protege la trazabilidad.
Raquel decide.
La IA estructura, ejecuta y valida.
```

---

# 3. Alcance permitido

```yaml id="9bzk5w"
allowed_scope:
  - documentation_workflow_binding_context
  - conversation_history_preservation
  - project_state_preservation
  - source_classification
  - decision_tracking
  - verification_matrix_generation
  - checkpoint_governance
  - command_palette_governance
  - role_binding
  - portable_packet_handoff
  - rehydration_support
```

# 4. Alcance bloqueado

```yaml id="ekm9b4"
blocked_scope:
  - runtime_truth
  - implementation_truth
  - source_code_behavior_truth
  - deployment_truth
  - final_commit_without_approval
  - automatic_snapshot_approval
  - automatic_canon_promotion_of_ai_outputs
```

El safe export de PROMETHEUS conserva explícitamente el alcance documental/workflow/binding y mantiene `runtime_truth` e `implementation_truth` bloqueados. `08. ChatGPT-Exportación comprometida segura.md`

---

# 5. Principios nucleares

```yaml id="iub5qi"
principles:
  P01_source_first:
    statement: Toda afirmación debe tener fuente o estado explícito.

  P02_raw_logs_are_evidence:
    statement: Los raw logs son verdad para historial conversacional y decisiones declaradas.

  P03_ai_outputs_are_candidates:
    statement: Los outputs de IA no son canon automático.

  P04_no_destruction:
    statement: Ninguna consolidación elimina fuentes previas.

  P05_conflicts_are_registered:
    statement: Los conflictos se registran con fuente, no se resuelven unilateralmente.

  P06_checkpoint_before_commit:
    statement: No hay snapshot, commit o export final sin gates cumplidos.

  P07_materialized_or_not_promoted:
    statement: Lo referenciado no se trata como materializado.

  P08_portable_by_design:
    statement: Todo estado útil debe poder rehidratarse en otra sesión o modelo.
```

El principio de no destrucción hereda directamente de METANO_IA, y el patrón de merge no destructivo aparece también en el pipeline de memoria: extract → normalize → merge → import, agrupando duplicados y registrando contradicciones. `METANO_IA_universal_prompt.md` `memory_merge_pipeline.html`

---

# 6. Objetos canónicos

```yaml id="il3yt5"
canonical_objects:

  Source:
    definition: Archivo, log, chat, packet, prompt, dashboard, output o evidencia disponible.

  Decision:
    definition: Determinación tomada, propuesta o bloqueada, siempre con fuente.

  Claim:
    definition: Afirmación que requiere verificación, clasificación o bloqueo.

  Verification:
    definition: Estado que indica si un claim está respaldado, pendiente, bloqueado o en conflicto.

  Checkpoint:
    definition: Gate que impide promoción indebida a snapshot, commit, runtime o release.

  Command:
    definition: Acción invocable con estado safe, gated, control o blocked.

  Packet:
    definition: Contenedor portable de estado, fuentes, decisiones, comandos y locks.

  Binding:
    definition: Vínculo entre un core neutral y un proyecto específico.

  Role:
    definition: Responsabilidad asignada a chat, modelo, agente o subsistema.

  Conflict:
    definition: Tensión entre fuentes preservada sin resolución unilateral.
```

---

# 7. Source Registry Protocol

Todo material entra primero al registro de fuentes.

```yaml id="s29k2f"
source_registry_entry:
  source_id: SRC.SYSTEM.NUMBER
  title: string
  file: string | UNKNOWN
  source_class: raw_log | packet | prompt | artifact | audit | branch | missing
  authority_level:
    - primary_conversation_truth
    - committed_documentation_truth
    - authoritative_metadata
    - structured_candidate
    - returned_ai_verification_bundle
    - context_hint
    - none
  status:
    - accepted
    - candidate
    - branch_support
    - missing
    - quarantined
    - conflicted
  use_for: []
  not_use_for: []
  treatment: string
```

El `Packet Ingestion Ready` ya definía como outputs esperados `upload_source_registry`, `source_registry`, `decision_log`, `verification_matrix`, `checkpoint_lock`, `command_palette` y `next_action_menu`. `12. ChatGPT-Packet Ingestion Ready.md`

---

# 8. Truth Hierarchy Protocol

```yaml id="m1pc1b"
truth_hierarchy:
  rank_1_primary_sources:
    examples:
      - raw_logs_for_conversation_truth
      - original_files
      - source_code_when_available

  rank_2_authoritative_metadata:
    examples:
      - manifest
      - checksums
      - committed_packet
      - official_metadata

  rank_3_committed_documentation:
    examples:
      - README
      - release_notes
      - safe_export_packet

  rank_4_structured_candidates:
    examples:
      - generated_audit
      - candidate_summary
      - proposed_patch

  rank_5_returned_ai_outputs:
    examples:
      - Claude_review
      - GPT_rescan
      - Copilot_patch

  rank_6_context_memory:
    examples:
      - rehydrated_chat_context
      - memory_snapshot
      - continuity_hint
```

Regla:

```text id="e4lzmq"
Un output de IA puede orientar, pero no canoniza.
```

Los logs de auditoría y rescan insisten en que los outputs devueltos deben tratarse como candidatos salvo respaldo source-backed, array-backed o materializado. `02. ChatGPT-Copilot's Patch (latest release).md` `07. ChatGPT-Dictamen Prometheus Rescan.md`

---

# 9. Verification Matrix Protocol

```yaml id="g70wy8"
verification_matrix_entry:
  claim_id: CLAIM.SYSTEM.NUMBER
  claim: string
  source_ids: []
  verification_status:
    - verified
    - docs_confirmed_candidate
    - partial_candidate
    - returned_ai_verification_bundle
    - missing
    - conflicted
    - quarantined
    - blocked_runtime_truth
    - blocked_implementation_truth
  promotion_allowed: boolean
  required_next_action: string
```

Estados recomendados:

```yaml id="k6j8pu"
verification_statuses:
  verified:
    meaning: respaldado por fuente suficiente dentro de su alcance

  docs_confirmed_candidate:
    meaning: útil para documentación, no canon final

  returned_ai_verification_bundle:
    meaning: output de modelo externo que requiere revisión

  blocked_runtime_truth:
    meaning: no hay fuente suficiente para afirmar runtime

  blocked_implementation_truth:
    meaning: no hay fuente suficiente para afirmar implementación

  conflicted:
    meaning: hay tensión entre fuentes

  quarantined:
    meaning: no debe mezclarse hasta revisión
```

---

# 10. Checkpoint Lock Protocol

```yaml id="sghfbi"
checkpoint_lock:
  current_phase: string
  snapshot_approval: BLOCKED | APPROVED
  final_commit: BLOCKED | APPROVED
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED | VERIFIED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED | VERIFIED

  gates:
    source_registry_exists: PASS | FAIL
    decision_log_exists: PASS | FAIL
    verification_matrix_exists: PASS | FAIL
    manifest_exists: PASS | FAIL
    materialization_audit_passed: PASS | FAIL
    user_snapshot_approval: PASS | FAIL
    user_final_commit_approval: PASS | FAIL
```

Estados base:

```yaml id="snu7y5"
default_lock:
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED
  snapshot_approval: BLOCKED
  final_commit: BLOCKED
```

Los estados del proyecto ya mostraban que el core podía estar listo para ingestión, mientras snapshot approval y final commit seguían bloqueados. `11. ChatGPT-Estado del Proyecto.md`

---

# 11. Materialization Audit Protocol

```yaml id="w9lk2o"
materialization_audit:
  requirement: >
    Ningún patch, array, registry, manifest o claim estructural
    se promueve solo porque está mencionado.

  must_be:
    - standalone
    - parseable
    - counted
    - classified
    - linked_in_manifest
    - linked_in_ingest_block
```

Regla corta:

```text id="s3mtw5"
Referenced ≠ materialized.
Listed ≠ linked.
Linked ≠ verified.
```

El Dictamen Prometheus Rescan declara que no basta con que un patch esté listado; debe estar materializado como archivo/array standalone, parseable, contado, clasificado y enlazado en manifest + ingest block. `07. ChatGPT-Dictamen Prometheus Rescan.md`

---

# 12. Non-Destructive Merge Protocol

```yaml id="y6cear"
non_destructive_merge:
  preserve_all_sources: true
  delete_duplicates: false
  group_semantic_duplicates: true
  keep_lineage: true
  conflict_register_required: true
  unilateral_resolution_allowed: false
```

Flujo:

```text id="7lsomr"
extract
→ normalize
→ compare
→ group duplicates
→ register conflicts
→ preserve source lineage
→ produce unified view
```

Este patrón ya está implementado conceptualmente en `memory_merge_pipeline.html`, que normaliza exports, deduplica equivalentes semánticos y registra contradicciones sin borrar datos. `memory_merge_pipeline.html`

---

# 13. Rescue Before Delete Protocol

```yaml id="mm0yug"
rescue_before_delete:
  command: /RESCUE-BEFORE-DELETE
  purpose: >
    Preservar todo contenido útil antes de eliminar un proyecto,
    chat, workspace o contexto activo.

  sequence:
    - inventory
    - extract
    - classify
    - dedupe
    - map_modules
    - audit
    - packetize
    - handoff
```

Output mínimo:

```yaml id="b3svuh"
rescue_outputs:
  - source_registry
  - decision_log
  - verification_matrix
  - checkpoint_lock
  - command_palette
  - manifest
  - portable_bundle
```

---

# 14. Portable Packet Ingestion Protocol

```yaml id="kc6tjf"
portable_packet:
  required_sections:
    metadata: required
    install_instruction: required
    active_scope: required
    blocked_scope: required
    source_registry: required
    decision_log: required
    verification_matrix: required
    checkpoint_lock: required
    command_palette: required
    next_action_menu: required
```

Rehydration command:

```text id="zul6yq"
REHYDRATE | SYSTEM="PROMETHEUS" | MODE="PORTABLE_PACKET_INGESTION" | SCOPE="documentation_workflow_binding_context"

Install this packet as a governance, documentation, source-registry, verification-matrix, checkpoint-lock and handoff backbone.

Do not treat this packet as runtime truth, implementation truth, deployment truth or final commit approval.
```

El paquete portable `v1.2.1` ya estaba diseñado para ingestión en Claude, GPT, Gemini, Perplexity y otros modelos. `12. ChatGPT-Packet Ingestion Ready.md`

---

# 15. Role Binding Protocol

```yaml id="bsyf6v"
role_binding:
  chat_id_schema: REQUIRED
  role_taxonomy: REQUIRED
  scope_lock_rules: REQUIRED
  activation_phrase_format: REQUIRED
  non_use_policy: REQUIRED
  drift_quarantine_rules: REQUIRED
```

Reglas:

```yaml id="p7p6eb"
role_binding_rules:
  - every_chat_or_agent_gets_id
  - every_id_gets_scope
  - every_scope_gets_non_use_policy
  - no_role_absorbs_parent_system
  - no_cross_context_merge_without_permission
  - drift_goes_to_quarantine
```

El Role Binding Generator nace precisamente para crear IDs, roles, scopes, gates y responsabilidades sin fusionar contextos no relacionados. `09. ChatGPT-Role Binding Generator.md`

---

# 16. Command Palette Governance Protocol

```yaml id="six38d"
command_palette:
  safe_commands:
    - /rehydrate
    - /inventory
    - /classify
    - /brief
    - /decision-log
    - /command-palette
    - /next

  gated_commands:
    - /verify
    - /approve-dry-run
    - /dry-run-merge
    - /review-returned-verification
    - /approve-snapshot
    - /approve-commit
    - /commit
    - /export

  control_commands:
    - /main-chat-exhaustive-rescan
    - /array-backed-merge-check
    - /patch-materialization-fixes
    - /source-backing-intake

  blocked_by_default:
    - /runtime-unlock
    - /implementation-unlock
    - /force-final-commit
```

Los estados del proyecto ya registraban una command palette con comandos sin aprobación, comandos con aprobación y rutas hacia inventory/classify/brief/dry-run/verify/commit. `11. ChatGPT-Estado del Proyecto.md` `13. ChatGPT-Rama! Estado del Proyecto.md`

---

# 17. Standard workflow

```text id="sw6pei"
1. /rehydrate
2. /inventory
3. /classify
4. /dedupe
5. /decision-log
6. /verification-matrix
7. /checkpoint-lock
8. /materialization-audit
9. /packetize
10. /handoff
11. /approve-snapshot only if user approves
12. /commit only if all gates pass
```

# Veredicto

**PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1 queda redactado.**

No es release final.  
No es runtime.  
No es implementación.  
Sí es ya un **protocolo operativo reusable** para gobernar rescate, verificación, packetización y rehidratación de proyectos dentro de METANO_IA o fuera de él.

Siguiente movimiento recomendado:

```text id="eedqnd"
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-SCHEMAS
```

Ahí convertiríamos este draft en esquemas concretos:

```text id="xnd2ur"
source_registry.schema.yaml
decision_log.schema.yaml
verification_matrix.schema.yaml
checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml
command_palette.schema.yaml
portable_packet.schema.yaml
```

## Prompt:
23/6/2026, 5:01:14 a.m.

/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-SCHEMAS

## Response:
23/6/2026, 5:01:20 a.m.

Ejecutado:

```text
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-SCHEMAS
```

```yaml
command_result:
  command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-SCHEMAS
  phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2
  status: SCHEMAS_DRAFTED
  artifact_family: PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_SCHEMAS_v0.1
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  commit_status: NOT_COMMITTED
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED
  final_release: false
```

Estos esquemas convierten el draft del protocolo en **estructuras parseables**. La base viene de los outputs esperados del packet portable: `source_registry`, `decision_log`, `verification_matrix`, `checkpoint_lock`, `command_palette` y `next_action_menu`. `12. ChatGPT-Packet Ingestion Ready.md` También heredan la regla METANO_IA de no destrucción y registro de conflictos con fuente. `METANO_IA_universal_prompt.md`

# PROMETHEUS Protocol Core Schemas v0.1

## 1. `source_registry.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.SOURCE_REGISTRY.001
schema_name: source_registry
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - source_id
  - title
  - source_class
  - authority_level
  - status
  - use_for
  - not_use_for

fields:
  source_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^SRC\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "SRC.PROM.008"

  title:
    type: string
    example: "Exportación comprometida segura"

  file:
    type:
      - string
      - "UNKNOWN"
    example: "08. ChatGPT-Exportación comprometida segura.md"

  source_class:
    type: enum
    values:
      - raw_log
      - committed_packet
      - prompt
      - html_artifact
      - audit_candidate
      - branch_log
      - missing_sequence_item
      - returned_ai_output
      - authoritative_metadata
      - source_code
      - external_reference

  authority_level:
    type: enum
    values:
      - primary_conversation_truth
      - committed_documentation_truth
      - authoritative_metadata_truth
      - source_code_truth
      - structured_candidate
      - returned_ai_verification_bundle
      - context_hint
      - none

  status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - accepted
      - candidate
      - branch_support
      - missing
      - conflicted
      - quarantined
      - deprecated

  use_for:
    type: array
    items: string

  not_use_for:
    type: array
    items: string

  treatment:
    type: string
    example: "preserve_as_branch_validation"

validation_rules:
  - source_id_must_be_unique
  - missing_sources_must_use_status_missing
  - raw_logs_may_support_conversation_truth_only
  - source_code_truth_requires_actual_source_code
  - no_source_may_be_used_outside_not_use_for_boundary
```

---

## 2. `decision_log.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.DECISION_LOG.001
schema_name: decision_log
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - decision_id
  - decision
  - status
  - evidence
  - scope

fields:
  decision_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^DEC\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "DEC.PROM.P1.004"

  decision:
    type: string
    example: "v1.3.9.4.3-rescan-control-rc1 is the safe base."

  status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - proposed
      - accepted
      - locked
      - conditional
      - rejected
      - superseded
      - blocked

  evidence:
    type: array
    items:
      type: string
      pattern: "^SRC\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"

  scope:
    type: enum
    values:
      - documentation_workflow_binding_context
      - runtime_truth
      - implementation_truth
      - source_code_behavior
      - deployment_truth
      - final_commit
      - memory_context
      - protocol_design

  owner:
    type: enum
    values:
      - user
      - assistant
      - system
      - unknown

  approval_required:
    type: boolean

  promotion_allowed:
    type: boolean

  notes:
    type: string

validation_rules:
  - every_decision_requires_at_least_one_source
  - locked_decisions_cannot_be_overridden_without_new_decision
  - runtime_or_implementation_decisions_require_source_code_or_authoritative_metadata
  - final_commit_decision_requires_user_approval
```

---

## 3. `verification_matrix.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.VERIFICATION_MATRIX.001
schema_name: verification_matrix
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - claim_id
  - claim
  - source_ids
  - verification_status
  - promotion_allowed

fields:
  claim_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^CLAIM\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "CLAIM.PROM.RUNTIME.001"

  claim:
    type: string
    example: "Runtime truth remains blocked."

  source_ids:
    type: array
    items:
      type: string
      pattern: "^SRC\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"

  verification_status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - verified
      - docs_confirmed_candidate
      - partial_candidate
      - returned_ai_verification_bundle
      - missing
      - conflicted
      - quarantined
      - blocked_runtime_truth
      - blocked_implementation_truth
      - blocked_deployment_truth

  promotion_allowed:
    type: boolean

  required_next_action:
    type: string
    example: "/source-backing-intake"

  blocked_reason:
    type: string

  reviewed_by:
    type: string

validation_rules:
  - verified_claims_require_source_ids
  - returned_ai_outputs_default_to_candidate
  - blocked_runtime_truth_cannot_promote
  - blocked_implementation_truth_cannot_promote
  - conflicted_claims_require_conflict_register_entry
```

---

## 4. `checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.CHECKPOINT_LOCK.001
schema_name: checkpoint_lock
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - lock_id
  - current_phase
  - runtime_truth
  - implementation_truth
  - snapshot_approval
  - final_commit
  - gates

fields:
  lock_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^LOCK\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "LOCK.PROM.PHASE2.001"

  current_phase:
    type: string
    example: "PROMETHEUS.PHASE2.SCHEMAS"

  runtime_truth:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - VERIFIED
      - NOT_APPLICABLE

  implementation_truth:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - VERIFIED
      - NOT_APPLICABLE

  snapshot_approval:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - APPROVED
      - NOT_REQUESTED

  final_commit:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - APPROVED
      - NOT_REQUESTED

  gates:
    type: object
    required:
      - source_registry_exists
      - decision_log_exists
      - verification_matrix_exists
      - manifest_exists
      - materialization_audit_passed
      - user_snapshot_approval
      - user_final_commit_approval

    gate_status_values:
      - PASS
      - FAIL
      - PARTIAL
      - BLOCKED
      - NOT_STARTED
      - NOT_APPLICABLE

validation_rules:
  - final_commit_requires_all_required_gates_pass
  - snapshot_approval_requires_user_snapshot_approval_pass
  - runtime_truth_verified_requires_source_code_or_authoritative_runtime_metadata
  - implementation_truth_verified_requires_source_code_or_authoritative_implementation_metadata
```

---

## 5. `command_palette.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.COMMAND_PALETTE.001
schema_name: command_palette
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - command_id
  - command
  - class
  - approval_required
  - allowed_scope

fields:
  command_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^CMD\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "CMD.PROM.001"

  command:
    type: string
    example: "/inventory"

  class:
    type: enum
    values:
      - safe
      - gated
      - control
      - blocked

  approval_required:
    type: boolean

  allowed_scope:
    type: array
    items:
      type: enum
      values:
        - documentation_workflow_binding_context
        - source_registry
        - verification
        - checkpoint
        - packetization
        - role_binding
        - runtime_truth
        - implementation_truth
        - final_commit

  blocked_if:
    type: array
    items: string

  expected_outputs:
    type: array
    items: string

validation_rules:
  - blocked_commands_cannot_execute_without_override_decision
  - final_commit_commands_require_explicit_user_approval
  - runtime_unlock_commands_require_source_backing
  - safe_commands_must_not_promote_truth
```

Canonical command classes:

```yaml
command_classes:
  safe:
    - /rehydrate
    - /inventory
    - /classify
    - /brief
    - /decision-log
    - /command-palette
    - /next

  gated:
    - /verify
    - /approve-dry-run
    - /dry-run-merge
    - /review-returned-verification
    - /approve-snapshot
    - /approve-commit
    - /commit
    - /export

  control:
    - /main-chat-exhaustive-rescan
    - /array-backed-merge-check
    - /patch-materialization-fixes
    - /source-backing-intake

  blocked:
    - /runtime-unlock
    - /implementation-unlock
    - /force-final-commit
```

---

## 6. `materialization_audit.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.MATERIALIZATION_AUDIT.001
schema_name: materialization_audit
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - artifact_id
  - artifact_name
  - referenced
  - materialized
  - parseable
  - counted
  - classified
  - manifest_linked
  - ingest_block_linked
  - promotion_status

fields:
  artifact_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^ART\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "ART.PROM.ARRAY.001"

  artifact_name:
    type: string
    example: "source_registry.yaml"

  artifact_type:
    type: enum
    values:
      - manifest
      - registry
      - array
      - packet
      - markdown
      - yaml
      - json
      - html
      - script
      - unknown

  referenced:
    type: boolean

  materialized:
    type: boolean

  parseable:
    type: boolean

  counted:
    type: boolean

  classified:
    type: boolean

  manifest_linked:
    type: boolean

  ingest_block_linked:
    type: boolean

  promotion_status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - promotable
      - candidate
      - blocked
      - missing
      - quarantined

validation_rules:
  - referenced_alone_is_not_promotable
  - materialized_false_blocks_promotion
  - parseable_false_blocks_promotion
  - manifest_linked_false_blocks_release
  - ingest_block_linked_false_blocks_ingestion
```

Regla troncal:

```text
Referenced ≠ materialized.
Listed ≠ linked.
Linked ≠ verified.
```

Esta regla viene directamente de la línea de rescan/materialization audit, donde se exige que patches, arrays y registries estén materializados, parseables, contados, clasificados y enlazados. `07. ChatGPT-Dictamen Prometheus Rescan.md`

---

## 7. `portable_packet.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.PORTABLE_PACKET.001
schema_name: portable_packet
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_sections:
  - metadata
  - install_instruction
  - active_scope
  - blocked_scope
  - source_registry
  - decision_log
  - verification_matrix
  - checkpoint_lock
  - command_palette
  - next_action_menu

metadata:
  required_fields:
    - packet_id
    - packet_version
    - schema_version
    - created_at
    - status
    - parent_system
    - runtime_truth
    - implementation_truth
    - final_commit

fields:
  packet_id:
    type: string
    example: "prometheus_protocol_core_packet"

  packet_version:
    type: string
    example: "v0.1"

  schema_version:
    type: string
    example: "0.1"

  status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - draft
      - candidate
      - committed_docs_workflow_binding
      - frozen
      - deprecated

  parent_system:
    type: string
    example: "METANO_IA"

  active_scope:
    type: array
    items: string

  blocked_scope:
    type: array
    items: string

  runtime_truth:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - VERIFIED
      - NOT_APPLICABLE

  implementation_truth:
    type: enum
    values:
      - BLOCKED
      - VERIFIED
      - NOT_APPLICABLE

  final_commit:
    type: boolean

validation_rules:
  - packet_must_include_all_required_sections
  - packet_status_frozen_requires_checksums
  - committed_packet_requires_checkpoint_lock_pass
  - runtime_truth_verified_requires_runtime_evidence
  - implementation_truth_verified_requires_implementation_evidence
```

---

## 8. `role_binding.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.ROLE_BINDING.001
schema_name: role_binding
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - role_id
  - role_name
  - scope
  - authority
  - non_use_policy
  - drift_policy

fields:
  role_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^ROLE\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "ROLE.PROM.RESCAN.001"

  role_name:
    type: string
    example: "rescan_control_guardian"

  assigned_to:
    type: enum
    values:
      - chat
      - model
      - assistant
      - user
      - subsystem
      - external_tool

  scope:
    type: array
    items: string

  authority:
    type: array
    items:
      type: enum
      values:
        - observe
        - classify
        - verify
        - suggest
        - execute_docs_workflow
        - approve
        - commit

  non_use_policy:
    type: array
    items: string

  drift_policy:
    type: enum
    values:
      - quarantine
      - reject
      - ask_user
      - log_only

validation_rules:
  - every_role_requires_scope
  - every_role_requires_non_use_policy
  - commit_authority_requires_user_role
  - drift_must_not_auto_merge
```

El `Role Binding Generator` existe para mantener IDs estrictos, scope locks, non-use policy y drift quarantine. `09. ChatGPT-Role Binding Generator.md`

---

## 9. `conflict_register.schema.yaml`

```yaml
schema_id: PROMETHEUS.SCHEMA.CONFLICT_REGISTER.001
schema_name: conflict_register
version: 0.1
status: draft

required_fields:
  - conflict_id
  - conflict_type
  - field
  - variants
  - resolution_status

fields:
  conflict_id:
    type: string
    pattern: "^CONFLICT\\.[A-Z0-9_]+\\.[0-9]{3}$"
    example: "CONFLICT.PROM.VERSION.001"

  conflict_type:
    type: enum
    values:
      - contradiction
      - duplicate_branch
      - missing_source
      - version_conflict
      - scope_conflict
      - authority_conflict
      - runtime_claim_conflict

  field:
    type: string
    example: "packet_version"

  variants:
    type: array
    items:
      required:
        - source_id
        - content
      fields:
        source_id:
          type: string
        content:
          type: string

  resolution_status:
    type: enum
    values:
      - unresolved
      - resolved_by_user
      - resolved_by_source_hierarchy
      - quarantined
      - preserved_as_parallel

  treatment:
    type: string

validation_rules:
  - conflicts_must_preserve_all_variants
  - unresolved_conflicts_cannot_be_promoted
  - user_decision_required_for_content_conflicts
  - source_hierarchy_may_resolve_authority_conflicts_only
```

# `manifest.yaml`

```yaml id="m4jsa4"
manifest:
  manifest_id: MANIFEST.PROMETHEUS.PROTOCOL_CORE.001
  schema_version: 0.1
  protocol_id: PROMETHEUS.PROTOCOL.CORE.001
  protocol_name: PROMETHEUS Protocol Core
  protocol_version: 0.1
  status: draft_operational

  created_context:
    parent_system: METANO_IA
    subsystem: PROMETHEUS
    phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2
    generated_by_command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MANIFEST
    language: es-MX
    timezone_context: America/Monterrey

  purpose: >
    Definir el manifiesto operativo del PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1
    como subsistema de gobernanza, preservación, verificación,
    packetización y rehidratación dentro del ecosistema METANO_IA.

  system_boundary:
    parent_system:
      id: METANO_IA
      role: personal_orchestration_framework
      governs:
        - project_continuity
        - memory_regeneration
        - role_division
        - iterative_execution
        - non_destructive_workflow

    subsystem:
      id: PROMETHEUS
      role: governance_preservation_verification_packetization_subsystem
      governs:
        - source_registry
        - truth_hierarchy
        - verification_matrix
        - checkpoint_lock
        - materialization_audit
        - command_palette
        - role_binding
        - portable_packet_ingestion
        - rescue_before_delete

  authority:
    user:
      name: Raquel Alejandra
      role:
        - concept_owner
        - content_owner
        - final_decision_authority
        - approval_authority

    assistant:
      role:
        - structure
        - architecture
        - validation
        - normalization
        - protocol_drafting
        - technical_execution

  active_scope:
    - documentation_workflow_binding_context
    - conversation_history_preservation
    - source_governance
    - decision_tracking
    - verification_governance
    - checkpoint_governance
    - command_governance
    - role_binding
    - packetization
    - rehydration_handoff

  blocked_scope:
    - runtime_truth
    - implementation_truth
    - source_code_behavior_truth
    - deployment_truth
    - automatic_snapshot_approval
    - automatic_final_commit
    - automatic_canon_promotion_of_ai_outputs

  truth_boundary:
    raw_logs_are_sot_for:
      - conversation_history
      - prompts
      - responses
      - declared_decisions
      - command_sequence
      - documented_governance_state
      - packet_lineage_as_declared

    raw_logs_are_not_sot_for:
      - runtime_behavior
      - implementation_behavior
      - deployment_state
      - source_code_truth
      - external_factual_claims_without_verification

    default_truth_locks:
      runtime_truth: BLOCKED
      implementation_truth: BLOCKED
      deployment_truth: BLOCKED
      final_commit: BLOCKED
```

# Hydration order

```yaml id="spklfj"
hydration:
  hydration_id: HYDRATE.PROMETHEUS.PROTOCOL_CORE.001
  mode: progressive
  order:
    H0_manifest:
      load:
        - manifest.yaml
      purpose: establish identity, scope and locks

    H1_parent_context:
      load:
        - METANO_IA_boundary
        - system_boundary
      purpose: prevent Prometheus from absorbing the parent system

    H2_schemas:
      load:
        - schemas/source_registry.schema.yaml
        - schemas/decision_log.schema.yaml
        - schemas/verification_matrix.schema.yaml
        - schemas/checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml
        - schemas/command_palette.schema.yaml
        - schemas/materialization_audit.schema.yaml
        - schemas/portable_packet.schema.yaml
        - schemas/role_binding.schema.yaml
        - schemas/conflict_register.schema.yaml
      purpose: define parseable structures

    H3_registries:
      load:
        - registries/source_registry.yaml
        - registries/decision_log.yaml
        - registries/verification_matrix.yaml
        - registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml
        - registries/command_palette.yaml
      purpose: restore operational state

    H4_controls:
      load:
        - registries/materialization_audit.yaml
        - registries/conflict_register.yaml
        - registries/role_binding_registry.yaml
      purpose: prevent false promotion, role drift and destructive merge

    H5_packet_body:
      load:
        - README.md
        - protocol_core.md
        - portable_ingestion_card.md
      purpose: restore human-readable protocol

    H6_next_actions:
      load:
        - next_action_menu.yaml
      purpose: continue work safely
```

  registry_id: PROMETHEUS.DECISION_LOG.001
  version: 0.1
  status: draft_seed

  decisions:

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.001
      decision: PROMETHEUS is a subsystem of METANO_IA, not the parent system.
      status: accepted
      scope: protocol_design
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.METANOIA.001
        - SRC.METANOIA.003
        - SRC.PROM.012
      notes: >
        METANO_IA governs ecosystem continuity. PROMETHEUS governs
        evidence, verification, packetization and handoff.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.002
      decision: Raw logs are SoT for conversation history and declared governance.
      status: accepted
      scope: documentation_workflow_binding_context
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.001
        - SRC.PROM.004
        - SRC.PROM.011
      notes: >
        Raw logs preserve what was said, decided, sequenced and declared.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.003
      decision: Raw logs do not unlock runtime truth or implementation truth.
      status: locked
      scope: runtime_truth
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: true
      promotion_allowed: false
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.008
        - SRC.PROM.010
      notes: >
        Runtime and implementation claims require source code or authoritative metadata.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.004
      decision: v1.3.9.4.3-rescan-control-rc1 is the safe base for documentation/workflow/binding.
      status: accepted
      scope: documentation_workflow_binding_context
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.008
        - SRC.PROM.004
        - SRC.PROM.014
      notes: >
        This safe base does not unlock runtime, implementation or deployment truth.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.005
      decision: v1.3.10.x remains a conditional audit candidate.
      status: conditional
      scope: documentation_workflow_binding_context
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: true
      promotion_allowed: false
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.002
        - SRC.PROM.010
      notes: >
        Requires materialization audit before promotion.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.006
      decision: Branch logs are preserved as supporting evidence, not separate canon.
      status: accepted
      scope: protocol_design
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.013
        - SRC.PROM.014
      notes: >
        Dedupe by meaning; preserve lineage.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.007
      decision: No consolidation may delete prior data.
      status: locked
      scope: protocol_design
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.METANOIA.001
        - SRC.METANOIA.002
      notes: >
        Conflicts are registered with source; no unilateral deletion.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.008
      decision: Referenced artifacts are not promotable unless materialized, parseable, counted, classified and linked.
      status: locked
      scope: documentation_workflow_binding_context
      owner: assistant
      approval_required: false
      promotion_allowed: true
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.007
        - SRC.PROM.004
      notes: >
        Referenced does not equal materialized.

    - decision_id: DEC.PROM.009
      decision: Final commit and snapshot approval remain blocked.
      status: locked
      scope: final_commit
      owner: user
      approval_required: true
      promotion_allowed: false
      evidence:
        - SRC.PROM.008
        - SRC.PROM.011
        - SRC.PROM.012
      notes: >
        Requires explicit Raquel approval and passing gates.
```

La decisión de mantener `v1.3.9.4.3-rescan-control-rc1` como base segura se apoya en el safe export, que lo limita a documentación/workflow/binding y conserva `runtime_truth` e `implementation_truth` bloqueados. `08. ChatGPT-Exportación comprometida segura.md` La línea `v1.3.10.x` queda condicional por sus pendientes de auditoría y readiness. `02. ChatGPT-Copilot's Patch (latest release).md`

# 5. `registries/command_palette.yaml`

```yaml
command_palette:
  palette_id: PROMETHEUS.COMMAND_PALETTE.001
  version: 0.1
  status: draft_seed

  commands:

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.001
      command: /rehydrate
      class: safe
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - documentation_workflow_binding_context
      expected_outputs:
        - restored_context
        - active_scope
        - checkpoint_lock_summary

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.002
      command: /inventory
      class: safe
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - source_registry
      expected_outputs:
        - upload_source_registry
        - source_registry_seed
        - missing_sources_report

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.003
      command: /classify
      class: safe
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - source_registry
        - verification
      expected_outputs:
        - classified_sources
        - authority_levels
        - use_boundaries

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.004
      command: /decision-log
      class: safe
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - documentation_workflow_binding_context
      expected_outputs:
        - decision_log

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.005
      command: /checkpoint-lock
      class: control
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - checkpoint
      expected_outputs:
        - checkpoint_lock
        - gate_status

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.006
      command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT
      class: control
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - packetization
        - verification
      expected_outputs:
        - materialization_audit
        - blocked_artifacts
        - promotable_artifacts

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.007
      command: /review-returned-verification
      class: gated
      approval_required: false
      allowed_scope:
        - verification
      expected_outputs:
        - returned_bundle_classification
        - merge_recommendation
      blocked_if:
        - no_source_registry

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.008
      command: /approve-snapshot
      class: gated
      approval_required: true
      allowed_scope:
        - final_commit
      expected_outputs:
        - snapshot_approval_record
      blocked_if:
        - materialization_audit_not_passed
        - unresolved_conflicts
        - no_user_approval

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.009
      command: /commit
      class: gated
      approval_required: true
      allowed_scope:
        - final_commit
      expected_outputs:
        - committed_packet
        - release_notes
        - checksums
      blocked_if:
        - no_user_approval
        - runtime_truth_conflict
        - implementation_truth_conflict
        - materialization_audit_not_passed

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.010
      command: /export
      class: gated
      approval_required: true
      allowed_scope:
        - packetization
      expected_outputs:
        - export_bundle
        - manifest
        - checksums
      blocked_if:
        - no_manifest
        - no_checksums
        - no_checkpoint_pass

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.011
      command: /runtime-unlock
      class: blocked
      approval_required: true
      allowed_scope:
        - runtime_truth
      expected_outputs: []
      blocked_if:
        - no_primary_source_code
        - no_authoritative_runtime_metadata

    - command_id: CMD.PROM.012
      command: /implementation-unlock
      class: blocked
      approval_required: true
      allowed_scope:
        - implementation_truth
      expected_outputs: []
      blocked_if:
        - no_source_code
        - no_authoritative_implementation_metadata

  next_safe_command:
    command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT
```

  "final_commit": false,
  "snapshot_approval": false,

}
```

Siguiente comando seguro:

```text
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT
```

Ese paso revisa si estos registros ya pueden considerarse **materializados** o si siguen como `draft_text_only`.

## Prompt:
23/6/2026, 2:05:05 p.m.

/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT

## Response:
23/6/2026, 2:05:13 p.m.

Ejecuto auditoría estricta: distinguiré entre “existe en el chat como borrador” y “existe como artefacto standalone parseable”. La meta es evitar una falsa promoción del protocolo.

Ejecutado:

```text id="i096ew"
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT
```

```yaml id="rosh18"
command_result:
  command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT
  phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2
  status: FAIL_WITH_CANDIDATE_PROGRESS
  reason: >
    Los artefactos existen como draft textual dentro del chat,
    pero aún no están materializados como archivos standalone,
    parseables, firmados con checksum ni empaquetados.
  commit_status: BLOCKED
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED
  final_release: false
```

La regla aplicada viene de Prometheus: no basta con que un patch, array o registry esté listado; debe estar **materializado, parseable, contado, clasificado y enlazado** antes de promoción. `07. ChatGPT-Dictamen Prometheus Rescan.md`

# PROMETHEUS · Materialization Audit v0.1

## 1. Regla de auditoría

```yaml id="h27q8l"
materialization_rule:
  referenced: does_not_equal_materialized
  drafted_in_chat: does_not_equal_standalone_file
  standalone_file: does_not_equal_parseable
  parseable: does_not_equal_verified
  verified: does_not_equal_committed
```

Versión corta:

```text id="4t4hyd"
Referenced ≠ Materialized
Drafted ≠ Packaged
Packaged ≠ Verified
Verified ≠ Committed
```

Esto también respeta METANO_IA: consolidar no debe borrar fuentes ni resolver conflictos unilateralmente. `METANO_IA_universal_prompt.md`

# Veredicto

La auditoría **falló correctamente**. Eso es bueno.

Significa que Prometheus está funcionando: no permitió confundir “lo redactado en el chat” con “lo materializado como paquete portable”.

Siguiente comando seguro:

```text id="64nmqh"
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZE-MISSING
```

Ese paso debe generar los artefactos faltantes como texto standalone: `role_binding_registry.yaml`, `conflict_register.yaml`, `README.md`, `portable_ingestion_card.md` y `next_action_menu.yaml`.

## Prompt:
23/6/2026, 2:06:25 p.m.

/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZE-MISSING

## Response:
23/6/2026, 2:06:32 p.m.

Ejecutaré “materialize missing” como **materialización textual standalone**: crearé los archivos faltantes en bloques copiables. No afirmaré checksum ni archivo real hasta que existan fuera del chat.

Ejecutado:

```text
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZE-MISSING
```

```yaml
command_result:
  command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZE-MISSING
  phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2
  status: PASS_TEXTUAL_MATERIALIZATION
  artifact_family: PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1
  materialization_level: standalone_text_blocks
  filesystem_materialization: false
  checksum_status: NOT_STARTED
  runtime_truth: BLOCKED
  implementation_truth: BLOCKED
  final_commit: false
```

Los artefactos faltantes quedan materializados como **bloques standalone copiables**. Aún no son archivos reales con checksum; ese gate sigue bloqueado hasta escribirlos como archivos y validarlos. La estructura sigue el packet portable de Prometheus, que requiere registros, checkpoint lock, command palette y next action menu para rehidratación. `12. ChatGPT-Packet Ingestion Ready.md`

# 2. `registries/conflict_register.yaml`

```yaml
conflict_register:
  registry_id: PROMETHEUS.CONFLICT_REGISTER.001
  version: 0.1
  status: draft_seed
  policy:
    preserve_all_variants: true
    no_unilateral_resolution: true
    unresolved_conflicts_block_final_commit: true
    source_hierarchy_may_resolve_authority_conflicts_only: true

  conflicts:

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.001
      conflict_type: missing_source
      field: source_sequence
      variants:
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.003
          content: "Numbered file 03 is missing or not provided."
      resolution_status: unresolved
      treatment: declare_missing_do_not_infer
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - full_lineage_certainty

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.002
      conflict_type: duplicate_branch
      field: project_status_log
      variants:
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.011
          content: "Estado del Proyecto functions as primary status log."
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.013
          content: "Rama Estado del Proyecto functions as branch or parallel support."
      resolution_status: preserved_as_parallel
      treatment: promote_SRC_PROM_011_as_primary_view_preserve_SRC_PROM_013_as_branch_support
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - independent_branch_canon

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.003
      conflict_type: duplicate_branch
      field: rescan_control_log
      variants:
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.004
          content: "Rescan Control Guardian functions as primary rescan-control source."
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.014
          content: "Rama Rescan Control Guardian functions as branch support."
      resolution_status: preserved_as_parallel
      treatment: promote_SRC_PROM_004_as_primary_view_preserve_SRC_PROM_014_as_branch_support
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - independent_branch_canon

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.004
      conflict_type: version_conflict
      field: active_packet_version
      variants:
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.008
          content: "v1.3.9.4.3-rescan-control-rc1 is the safe committed docs/workflow/binding base."
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.002
          content: "v1.3.10.x appears as next audit/release-readiness candidate."
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.010
          content: "v1.3.10.x remains conditional pending audit."
      resolution_status: resolved_by_source_hierarchy
      treatment: v1_3_9_4_3_is_safe_base_v1_3_10_x_is_candidate
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - v1_3_10_x_final_release

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.005
      conflict_type: scope_conflict
      field: runtime_truth
      variants:
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.008
          content: "Runtime truth is BLOCKED."
        - source_id: SRC.PROM.010
          content: "Technical audit references release readiness but keeps runtime/implementation blocked."
      resolution_status: resolved_by_source_hierarchy
      treatment: runtime_truth_remains_blocked
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - runtime_unlock
        - implementation_unlock
        - deployment_truth

    - conflict_id: CONFLICT.PROM.006
      conflict_type: materialization_conflict
      field: artifact_materialization
      variants:
        - source_id: derived_from_chat_outputs
          content: "Schemas, manifest and registries exist as chat text."
        - source_id: materialization_audit
          content: "They do not yet exist as standalone parseable files with checksums."
      resolution_status: unresolved
      treatment: textual_materialization_only_until_files_exist
      promotion_blocked_for:
        - candidate_package_release
        - final_export
        - checksums

  validation:
    status: draft
    required_next_action: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT-RERUN
```

El registro sigue la regla METANO_IA de no destruir ni resolver conflictos unilateralmente: se preservan variantes con fuente. `METANO_IA_universal_prompt.md`

# 3. `README.md`

```markdown
# PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1

**Status:** Draft operational  
**Parent system:** METANO_IA  
**Subsystem:** PROMETHEUS  
**Scope:** Documentation / workflow / binding governance  
**Runtime truth:** BLOCKED  
**Implementation truth:** BLOCKED  
**Final commit:** false  

## Purpose

PROMETHEUS Protocol Core is a governance, preservation, verification and packetization protocol for converting chats, raw logs, project files, decisions, branches, AI outputs and artifacts into portable, traceable and rehydratable project packets.

It is not a runtime engine, source-code implementation, deployment claim or final project commit.

## Parent Boundary

METANO_IA is the parent orchestration ecosystem.

PROMETHEUS is a subsystem inside METANO_IA responsible for:

- source registry
- truth hierarchy
- verification matrix
- checkpoint lock
- materialization audit
- command palette governance
- role binding
- rescue before delete
- portable packet ingestion
- rehydration handoff

## Core Principles

1. Source first.
2. Raw logs are evidence for conversation and declared governance.
3. AI outputs are candidates until verified.
4. No destructive merge.
5. Conflicts are registered with source.
6. Checkpoint before snapshot, commit or export.
7. Referenced is not materialized.
8. Portable by design.

## Required Package Structure

```text
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1/
├── manifest.yaml
├── README.md
├── protocol_core.md
├── portable_ingestion_card.md
├── schemas/
├── registries/
├── evidence/
└── release/
```

## Required Registries

- source_registry.yaml
- decision_log.yaml
- verification_matrix.yaml
- checkpoint_lock.yaml
- command_palette.yaml
- materialization_audit.yaml
- role_binding_registry.yaml
- conflict_register.yaml

## Current Status

The protocol, schemas, manifest and registry seeds have been drafted textually.

They are not yet filesystem-materialized, parse-verified, checksum-signed or committed.

## Next Safe Command

```text
/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-MATERIALIZATION-AUDIT-RERUN
```

## Blocked Commands

```text
/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT
/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT
/PROMETHEUS-RUNTIME-UNLOCK
/PROMETHEUS-IMPLEMENTATION-UNLOCK
/PROMETHEUS-EXPORT-FINAL
```
```

# 4. `portable_ingestion_card.md`

```markdown
# PROMETHEUS Portable Ingestion Card v0.1

Copy and paste this block at the beginning of a new session or AI environment.

```text
REHYDRATE | SYSTEM="PROMETHEUS" | PROTOCOL="PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE" | VERSION="0.1" | PARENT_SYSTEM="METANO_IA" | MODE="PORTABLE_PACKET_INGESTION"

Install PROMETHEUS as a governance, preservation, verification and packetization subsystem.

Scope:
- documentation_workflow_binding_context
- source_registry
- decision_log
- verification_matrix
- checkpoint_lock
- materialization_audit
- command_palette
- role_binding
- rescue_before_delete
- portable_handoff

Do not treat this packet as:
- runtime truth
- implementation truth
- deployment truth
- source-code truth
- final commit approval
- automatic snapshot approval

Truth policy:
- Raw logs are SoT for conversation history, prompts, responses, declared decisions, command sequence and documented governance state.
- Raw logs are not SoT for runtime behavior, implementation behavior or deployment.
- AI outputs remain candidates until source-backed, array-backed or materialized.
- Referenced artifacts are not promoted unless standalone, parseable, counted, classified and manifest-linked.

First actions:
1. Load manifest.
2. Load source_registry.
3. Load decision_log.
4. Load verification_matrix.
5. Load checkpoint_lock.
6. Load command_palette.
7. Load role_binding_registry.
8. Load conflict_register.
9. Return active scope, blocked scope, current gates and next action menu.

Default locks:
runtime_truth: BLOCKED
implementation_truth: BLOCKED
deployment_truth: BLOCKED
snapshot_approval: BLOCKED
final_commit: BLOCKED
```

```yaml
rehydration_report:
  system: PROMETHEUS
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  protocol_version: 0.1
  active_scope: []
  blocked_scope: []
  loaded_registries: []
  checkpoint_lock: {}
  conflicts: []
  next_action_menu: []
```
```

El formato deriva del portable packet ingestion pattern ya documentado en Prometheus, donde se esperaba instalar el packet como backbone para source registry, decision log, verification matrix y checkpoint lock. `12. ChatGPT-Packet Ingestion Ready.md`

      reason: no primary source code or authoritative runtime metadata

    - command: /PROMETHEUS-IMPLEMENTATION-UNLOCK

# PROMETHEUS · Materialization Audit Rerun v0.2

La auditoría mejora de **FAIL_WITH_CANDIDATE_PROGRESS** a:

```yaml
audit_verdict:
  textual_completeness: PASS
  registry_coverage: PASS_DRAFT
  standalone_file_materialization: FAIL

  checksums_generated: FAIL

```

La razón es consistente con la regla Prometheus: no basta con que un artefacto esté mencionado o redactado; debe estar materializado, parseable, contado, clasificado y enlazado antes de promoción. `07. ChatGPT-Dictamen Prometheus Rescan.md`

# 3. Promotion verdict

```yaml
promotion_verdict:
  promote_to_protocol_draft:
    allowed: true
    status: ALREADY_DRAFT

  promote_to_candidate_package:
    allowed: false
    blocker:
      - standalone_files_missing
      - parseability_not_verified
      - checksums_missing
      - materialization_report_missing

  promote_to_committed_packet:
    allowed: false
    blocker:
      - no_candidate_package
      - no_user_final_commit_approval
      - materialization_audit_not_passed

  unlock_runtime_truth:
    allowed: false
    blocker:
      - no_primary_source_code
      - no_authoritative_runtime_metadata

  unlock_implementation_truth:
    allowed: false
    blocker:
      - no_source_code
      - no_authoritative_implementation_metadata
```

Esto mantiene el alcance seguro original de Prometheus: documentación/workflow/binding, no runtime ni implementation truth. `08. ChatGPT-Exportación comprometida segura.md`

---

# 3. Orden de materialización

```yaml
materialization_order:

  stage_0_create_root:
    order: 0
    create:
      - PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1/

  stage_1_core_identity:
    order: 1
    create:
      - manifest.yaml
      - README.md
      - protocol_core.md
      - portable_ingestion_card.md
    reason: >
      Primero se materializa identidad, alcance, locks y lectura humana.

  stage_2_schemas:
    order: 2
    create:
      - schemas/source_registry.schema.yaml
      - schemas/decision_log.schema.yaml
      - schemas/verification_matrix.schema.yaml
      - schemas/checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml
      - schemas/command_palette.schema.yaml
      - schemas/materialization_audit.schema.yaml
      - schemas/portable_packet.schema.yaml
      - schemas/role_binding.schema.yaml
      - schemas/conflict_register.schema.yaml
    reason: >
      Los schemas deben existir antes de validar los registries.

  stage_3_registries:
    order: 3
    create:
      - registries/source_registry.yaml
      - registries/decision_log.yaml
      - registries/verification_matrix.yaml
      - registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml
      - registries/command_palette.yaml
      - registries/materialization_audit.yaml
      - registries/role_binding_registry.yaml
      - registries/conflict_register.yaml
      - registries/next_action_menu.yaml
    reason: >
      Los registries son el estado operativo rehidratable del paquete.

  stage_4_evidence_indexes:
    order: 4
    create:
      - evidence/raw_logs/README.raw_logs.md
      - evidence/raw_logs/source_index.yaml
      - evidence/metano_ia/README.metano_ia.md
      - evidence/metano_ia/source_index.yaml
      - evidence/branches/README.branches.md
      - evidence/branches/branch_index.yaml
    reason: >
      No se copian necesariamente todos los raw logs al paquete candidato;
      se indexan para trazabilidad y futura auditoría.

  stage_5_reports:
    order: 5
    create:
      - reports/package_inventory.yaml
      - reports/materialization_report.md
      - reports/parseability_report.md
      - reports/unresolved_gates.md
    reason: >
      Los reports declaran qué existe, qué valida y qué sigue bloqueado.

  stage_6_release_placeholders:
    order: 6
    create:
      - release/release_notes.md
      - release/candidate_export_status.yaml
    defer_until_checksum_stage:
      - release/checksums.sha256
    reason: >
      Checksums solo se generan después de que todos los archivos existan.
```

---

# 4. Reglas de naming

```yaml
naming_rules:
  package_root:
    pattern: "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v{MAJOR}.{MINOR}"
    current: "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1"

  yaml_files:
    casing: snake_case
    extension: ".yaml"
    rule: "machine-readable registries, schemas, reports and indexes"

  markdown_files:
    casing: snake_case_or_UPPERCASE_README
    extension: ".md"
    rule: "human-readable protocol, instructions and reports"

  schemas:
    pattern: "{object_name}.schema.yaml"

  registries:
    pattern: "{registry_name}.yaml"

  reports:
    pattern: "{report_name}.md or {report_name}.yaml"

  release:
    checksum_file: "checksums.sha256"
    release_notes: "release_notes.md"
```

---

# 5. Artefactos raíz

## 5.1 `manifest.yaml`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: manifest.yaml
  type: yaml
  required: true
  parse_required: true
  purpose: >
    Identidad del paquete, alcance, sistema padre, locks,
    hydration order, schemas, registries y estado de release.
  depends_on: []
  must_reference:
    - schemas/
    - registries/
    - reports/
    - release/
```

## 5.2 `README.md`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: README.md
  type: markdown
  required: true
  parse_required: false
  purpose: >
    Explicación humana del paquete, uso, límites, comandos bloqueados
    y siguiente acción segura.
  depends_on:
    - manifest.yaml
```

## 5.3 `protocol_core.md`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: protocol_core.md
  type: markdown
  required: true
  parse_required: false
  purpose: >
    Texto completo del PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1.
  depends_on:
    - manifest.yaml
```

## 5.4 `portable_ingestion_card.md`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: portable_ingestion_card.md
  type: markdown
  required: true
  parse_required: false
  purpose: >
    Tarjeta copy/paste para rehidratar PROMETHEUS en otra sesión o modelo.
  depends_on:
    - manifest.yaml
    - registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml
```

# 7. Registries requeridos

```yaml
registries_spec:
  root: registries/

  required_files:

    - path: registries/source_registry.yaml

    - path: registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml
      purpose: Locks de runtime, implementación, snapshot y commit.
      criticality: P0

    - path: registries/command_palette.yaml

      criticality: P1

    - path: registries/materialization_audit.yaml

      criticality: P0

    - path: registries/role_binding_registry.yaml
      purpose: Roles, scopes, autoridad, non-use policy y drift quarantine.
      criticality: P1

    - path: registries/conflict_register.yaml

      criticality: P0

    - path: registries/next_action_menu.yaml
      purpose: Secuencia de trabajo segura.
      criticality: P1
```

---

# 8. Evidence indexes

No vamos a duplicar todavía todos los raw logs dentro del paquete candidato, porque eso haría el paquete pesado y todavía no es release final.

En esta etapa se crean **índices de evidencia**.

```yaml
evidence_policy:
  copy_full_raw_logs_now: false
  create_source_indexes_now: true
  preserve_original_uploads_as_external_evidence: true
  reason: >
    El paquete candidato debe ser portable y auditable,
    sin inflarse con todos los logs hasta decidir si se hace full evidence bundle.
```

## 8.1 `evidence/raw_logs/source_index.yaml`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: evidence/raw_logs/source_index.yaml
  type: yaml
  purpose: >
    Índice de logs Prometheus SRC.PROM.001 a SRC.PROM.014,
    incluyendo missing SRC.PROM.003.
  required: true
```

## 8.2 `evidence/metano_ia/source_index.yaml`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: evidence/metano_ia/source_index.yaml
  type: yaml
  purpose: >
    Índice de fuentes METANO_IA usadas como contexto padre.
  required: true
```

## 8.3 `evidence/branches/branch_index.yaml`

```yaml
file_spec:
  path: evidence/branches/branch_index.yaml
  type: yaml
  purpose: >
    Índice de ramas preservadas: Estado/Rama Estado y Rescan/Rama Rescan.
  required: true
```

```yaml
reports_spec:

    - path: reports/materialization_report.md

    - path: reports/unresolved_gates.md
      type: markdown
      purpose: >
        Lista de gates que impiden candidate export, final commit,
        runtime unlock o implementation unlock.
```

# 12. Parseability acceptance criteria

```yaml
parseability_acceptance_criteria:
  yaml_files:
    requirement: all_yaml_files_must_parse
    applies_to:
      - manifest.yaml
      - schemas/*.yaml
      - registries/*.yaml
      - evidence/**/*.yaml
      - reports/package_inventory.yaml
      - release/candidate_export_status.yaml

  markdown_files:
    requirement: markdown_files_must_exist_and_be_non_empty
    applies_to:
      - README.md
      - protocol_core.md
      - portable_ingestion_card.md
      - evidence/**/*.md
      - reports/*.md
      - release/release_notes.md

  failure_policy:
    any_required_yaml_parse_failure: BLOCK_CANDIDATE_EXPORT
    missing_required_file: BLOCK_CANDIDATE_EXPORT
    empty_required_markdown: BLOCK_CANDIDATE_EXPORT
```

# 13. Checksum acceptance criteria

  algorithm: SHA-256
  include:
    - all_files_under_PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1

  exclude:
    - operating_system_hidden_files
    - temporary_files
    - editor_backup_files

  required_output:
    file: release/checksums.sha256
    format: "<sha256_hash>  <relative_file_path>"

  gate:
    checksums_generated:
      pass_condition: every_required_file_has_hash
      fail_condition:
        - missing_hash
        - duplicate_path
        - hash_generated_before_final_file_state
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import textwrap

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1")

def clean(value: str) -> str:
    return textwrap.dedent(value).strip() + "\n"

def write_file(relative_path: str, content: str) -> None:
    path = ROOT / relative_path
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(clean(content), encoding="utf-8")

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def main() -> None:
    ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    generated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

    files: dict[str, str] = {}

      protocol_version: "0.1"
      status: draft_operational
      generated_at: "{generated_at}"

      created_context:
        parent_system: METANO_IA
        subsystem: PROMETHEUS
        phase: PROMETHEUS.PHASE2.FILE_MATERIALIZE
        language: es-MX
        timezone_context: America/Monterrey

      authority:
        user:
          name: Raquel Alejandra
          role:
            - concept_owner
            - content_owner
            - final_decision_authority
            - approval_authority
        assistant:
          role:
            - structure
            - architecture
            - validation
            - normalization
            - protocol_drafting
            - technical_execution

      active_scope:
        - documentation_workflow_binding_context
        - conversation_history_preservation
        - source_governance
        - decision_tracking
        - verification_governance
        - checkpoint_governance
        - command_governance
        - role_binding
        - packetization
        - rehydration_handoff

      blocked_scope:
        - runtime_truth
        - implementation_truth
        - source_code_behavior_truth
        - deployment_truth
        - automatic_snapshot_approval
        - automatic_final_commit
        - automatic_canon_promotion_of_ai_outputs

      truth_boundary:
        raw_logs_are_sot_for:
          - conversation_history
          - prompts
          - responses
          - declared_decisions
          - command_sequence
          - documented_governance_state
          - packet_lineage_as_declared
        raw_logs_are_not_sot_for:
          - runtime_behavior
          - implementation_behavior
          - deployment_state
          - source_code_truth
          - external_factual_claims_without_verification

      default_truth_locks:
        runtime_truth: BLOCKED
        implementation_truth: BLOCKED
        deployment_truth: BLOCKED
        snapshot_approval: BLOCKED
        final_commit: BLOCKED

      package:
        package_id: PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1
        package_type: protocol_core_packet
        package_status: materialized_draft
        release_channel: internal_candidate_precheck
        final_commit: false
        snapshot_approval: false

      hydration_order:
        - manifest.yaml
        - schemas/
        - registries/source_registry.yaml
        - registries/decision_log.yaml
        - registries/verification_matrix.yaml
        - registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml
        - registries/command_palette.yaml
        - registries/materialization_audit.yaml
        - registries/role_binding_registry.yaml
        - registries/conflict_register.yaml
        - registries/next_action_menu.yaml
        - README.md
        - protocol_core.md
        - portable_ingestion_card.md
    """

    files["README.md"] = """
    # PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1

    **Status:** Materialized draft  
    **Parent system:** METANO_IA  
    **Subsystem:** PROMETHEUS  
    **Scope:** Documentation / workflow / binding governance  
    **Runtime truth:** BLOCKED  
    **Implementation truth:** BLOCKED  
    **Final commit:** false  

    ## Purpose

    PROMETHEUS Protocol Core is a governance, preservation, verification and packetization protocol for converting chats, raw logs, project files, decisions, branches, AI outputs and artifacts into portable, traceable and rehydratable project packets.

    It is not a runtime engine, source-code implementation, deployment claim or final project commit.

    ## Core Principles

    1. Source first.
    2. Raw logs are evidence for conversation and declared governance.
    3. AI outputs are candidates until verified.
    4. No destructive merge.
    5. Conflicts are registered with source.
    6. Checkpoint before snapshot, commit or export.
    7. Referenced is not materialized.
    8. Portable by design.

    ## Next Safe Command

    ```text
    /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-PARSEABILITY-CHECK
    ```

    ## Blocked Commands

    ```text
    /PROMETHEUS-COMMIT
    /PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT
    /PROMETHEUS-RUNTIME-UNLOCK
    /PROMETHEUS-IMPLEMENTATION-UNLOCK
    /PROMETHEUS-EXPORT-FINAL
    ```
    """

    files["protocol_core.md"] = """
    # PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1

    ## 1. System Boundary

    METANO_IA is the parent orchestration ecosystem.

    PROMETHEUS is a subsystem inside METANO_IA responsible for:

    - source registry
    - truth hierarchy
    - verification matrix
    - checkpoint lock
    - materialization audit
    - command palette governance
    - role binding
    - rescue before delete
    - portable packet ingestion
    - rehydration handoff

    ## 2. Allowed Scope

    - documentation workflow binding context
    - conversation history preservation
    - project state preservation
    - source classification
    - decision tracking
    - verification matrix generation
    - checkpoint governance
    - command palette governance
    - role binding
    - portable packet handoff
    - rehydration support

    ## 3. Blocked Scope

    - runtime truth
    - implementation truth
    - source code behavior truth
    - deployment truth
    - final commit without approval
    - automatic snapshot approval
    - automatic canon promotion of AI outputs

    ## 4. Core Protocol Modules

    ### Source Registry Protocol

    Every input source must be registered with authority level, status, allowed use and forbidden use.

    ### Truth Hierarchy Protocol

    AI outputs are candidates unless source-backed, array-backed, materialized and verified.

    ### Verification Matrix Protocol

    Every claim must have a verification state: verified, candidate, missing, conflicted, quarantined or blocked.

    ### Checkpoint Lock Protocol

    Runtime truth, implementation truth, snapshot approval and final commit remain blocked unless their gates pass.

    ### Materialization Audit Protocol

    Referenced is not materialized. Drafted is not packaged. Packaged is not verified. Verified is not committed.

    ### Non-Destructive Merge Protocol

    Consolidation preserves source lineage and registers conflicts without unilateral deletion.

    ### Rescue Before Delete Protocol

    Inventory, extract, classify, dedupe, map, audit, packetize and handoff before deleting or losing context.

    ### Portable Packet Ingestion Protocol

    A project state must be installable into another model or session using manifest, registries, locks and rehydration card.

    ### Role Binding Protocol

    Every chat, model, agent or subsystem gets an ID, scope, non-use policy and drift handling.

    ### Command Palette Governance Protocol

    Commands are classified as safe, gated, control or blocked.
    """

    files["portable_ingestion_card.md"] = """

    Scope:

    - Raw logs are not SoT for runtime behavior, implementation behavior or deployment.
    - AI outputs remain candidates until source-backed, array-backed or materialized.
    - Referenced artifacts are not promoted unless standalone, parseable, counted, classified and manifest-linked.

    First actions:

    Default locks:
    runtime_truth: BLOCKED
    implementation_truth: BLOCKED
    deployment_truth: BLOCKED
    snapshot_approval: BLOCKED
    final_commit: BLOCKED
    ```
