```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1")

EXPECTED_FILES = [
    "manifest.yaml",
    "README.md",
    "protocol_core.md",
    "portable_ingestion_card.md",

    "schemas/source_registry.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/decision_log.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/verification_matrix.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/command_palette.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/materialization_audit.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/portable_packet.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/role_binding.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/conflict_register.schema.yaml",

    "registries/source_registry.yaml",
    "registries/decision_log.yaml",
    "registries/verification_matrix.yaml",
    "registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml",
    "registries/command_palette.yaml",
    "registries/materialization_audit.yaml",
    "registries/role_binding_registry.yaml",
    "registries/conflict_register.yaml",
    "registries/next_action_menu.yaml",

    "evidence/raw_logs/README.raw_logs.md",
    "evidence/raw_logs/source_index.yaml",
    "evidence/metano_ia/README.metano_ia.md",
    "evidence/metano_ia/source_index.yaml",
    "evidence/branches/README.branches.md",
    "evidence/branches/branch_index.yaml",

    "reports/materialization_report.md",
    "reports/parseability_report.md",
    "reports/package_inventory.yaml",
    "reports/unresolved_gates.md",

    "release/checksums.sha256",
    "release/release_notes.md",
    "release/candidate_export_status.yaml",
]

YAML_SUFFIXES = (".yaml", ".yml")
MARKDOWN_SUFFIXES = (".md",)

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def sha256_text(text: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def load_yaml_module():
    try:
        import yaml  # type: ignore
        return yaml
    except Exception:
        print("ERROR: PyYAML is required for strict YAML parsing.")
        print("Install it with: python3 -m pip install pyyaml")
        sys.exit(2)

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def rebuild_package_inventory(yaml_module: Any, parse_results: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    inventory = {
        "package_inventory": {
            "inventory_id": "PROMETHEUS.PACKAGE_INVENTORY.001",
            "package_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1",
            "generated_at": now_iso(),
            "status": "parse_checked",
            "total_expected_files": len(EXPECTED_FILES),
            "files": [],
        }
    }

    result_by_path = {item["path"]: item for item in parse_results}

    for relative_path in EXPECTED_FILES:
        item = result_by_path.get(relative_path, {})
        inventory["package_inventory"]["files"].append(
            {
                "path": relative_path,
                "required": True,
                "exists": bool(item.get("exists")),
                "parse_required": relative_path.endswith(YAML_SUFFIXES),
                "parse_status": item.get("parse_status", "NOT_CHECKED"),
                "non_empty": bool(item.get("non_empty")),
                "checksum": {
                    "algorithm": "sha256",
                    "value": item.get("sha256"),
                },
                "promotion_status": (
                    "PROMOTABLE_TO_CHECKSUM_STAGE"
                    if item.get("exists") and item.get("parse_status") in {"PASS", "NOT_APPLICABLE"} and item.get("non_empty")
                    else "BLOCKED"
                ),
            }
        )

    write_text(ROOT / "reports/package_inventory.yaml", yaml_module.safe_dump(inventory, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module: Any, overall_pass: bool) -> None:
    checkpoint_path = ROOT / "registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml"
    if not checkpoint_path.exists():
        return

    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(checkpoint_path)) or {}
    lock = data.setdefault("checkpoint_lock", {})
    lock["current_phase"] = "PROMETHEUS.PHASE2.PARSEABILITY_CHECK"
    lock["status"] = "ACTIVE"

    gates = lock.setdefault("gates", {})
    gates["standalone_files_materialized"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["yaml_parseability_verified"] = {"status": "PASS" if overall_pass else "FAIL"}
    gates["markdown_package_verified"] = {"status": "PASS" if overall_pass else "FAIL"}
    gates["checksums_generated"] = {"status": "NOT_STARTED"}
    gates["candidate_export"] = {"status": "BLOCKED"}
    gates["final_commit"] = {"status": "BLOCKED"}

    lock["runtime_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["implementation_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["deployment_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["final_commit"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["next_safe_command"] = {
        "command": "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CHECKSUMS" if overall_pass else "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-PARSEABILITY-FIX",
        "reason": "Proceed only if all required files exist and parseability passes.",
    }

    write_text(checkpoint_path, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def create_parseability_report(parse_results: list[dict[str, Any]], overall_pass: bool) -> None:
    failures = [item for item in parse_results if not item["pass"]]

    lines = [
        "# Parseability Report",
        "",
        f"Generated at: `{now_iso()}`",
        "",
        f"Overall status: `{'PASS' if overall_pass else 'FAIL'}`",
        "",
        "## Summary",
        "",
        f"- Expected files: {len(EXPECTED_FILES)}",
        f"- Passed files: {len([item for item in parse_results if item['pass']])}",
        f"- Failed files: {len(failures)}",
        "",
        "## Truth Locks",
        "",
        "- runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- final_commit: BLOCKED",
        "",
    ]

    if failures:
        lines.extend(["## Failures", ""])
        for item in failures:
            lines.extend(
                [
                    f"### `{item['path']}`",
                    "",
                    f"- exists: `{item['exists']}`",
                    f"- parse_status: `{item['parse_status']}`",
                    f"- non_empty: `{item['non_empty']}`",
                    f"- error: `{item.get('error', 'none')}`",
                    "",
                ]
            )
    else:
        lines.extend(
            [
                "## Result",
                "",
                "All required files exist. All YAML files parse. All Markdown files are non-empty.",
                "",
                "Next safe command:",
                "",
                "```text",
                "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CHECKSUMS",
                "```",
                "",
            ]
        )

    write_text(ROOT / "reports/parseability_report.md", "\n".join(lines))

def check_package() -> int:
    yaml_module = load_yaml_module()

    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        print(f"FAIL: package root not found: {ROOT}")
        print("Run first: python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py")
        return 1

    parse_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for relative_path in EXPECTED_FILES:
        path = ROOT / relative_path
        exists = path.exists() and path.is_file()
        result: dict[str, Any] = {
            "path": relative_path,
            "exists": exists,
            "parse_status": "NOT_CHECKED",
            "non_empty": False,
            "sha256": None,
            "pass": False,
            "error": None,
        }

        if not exists:
            result["parse_status"] = "MISSING"
            result["error"] = "required file missing"
            parse_results.append(result)
            continue

        text = read_text(path)
        result["non_empty"] = bool(text.strip())
        result["sha256"] = sha256_text(text)

        if relative_path.endswith(YAML_SUFFIXES):
            try:
                yaml_module.safe_load(text)
                result["parse_status"] = "PASS"
            except Exception as exc:
                result["parse_status"] = "FAIL"
                result["error"] = str(exc).replace("\n", " ")
        elif relative_path.endswith(MARKDOWN_SUFFIXES):
            result["parse_status"] = "NOT_APPLICABLE"
            if not result["non_empty"]:
                result["error"] = "markdown file is empty"
        elif relative_path.endswith(".sha256"):
            result["parse_status"] = "NOT_APPLICABLE"
        else:
            result["parse_status"] = "NOT_APPLICABLE"

        result["pass"] = bool(
            result["exists"]
            and result["non_empty"]
            and result["parse_status"] in {"PASS", "NOT_APPLICABLE"}
        )
        parse_results.append(result)

    overall_pass = all(item["pass"] for item in parse_results)

    # Rebuild inventory after checking, because the first generator may have produced
    # a syntactically weak inventory. This makes the package inventory canonical.
    rebuild_package_inventory(yaml_module, parse_results)

    # Re-check package_inventory.yaml after rebuilding.
    inventory_path = ROOT / "reports/package_inventory.yaml"
    try:
        yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(inventory_path))
    except Exception as exc:
        overall_pass = False
        parse_results.append(
            {
                "path": "reports/package_inventory.yaml",
                "exists": True,
                "parse_status": "FAIL_AFTER_REBUILD",
                "non_empty": True,
                "sha256": sha256_text(read_text(inventory_path)),
                "pass": False,
                "error": str(exc).replace("\n", " "),
            }
        )

    update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module, overall_pass)
    create_parseability_report(parse_results, overall_pass)

    passed = len([item for item in parse_results if item["pass"]])
    failed = len([item for item in parse_results if not item["pass"]])

    print("PROMETHEUS parseability check complete.")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Expected files: {len(EXPECTED_FILES)}")
    print(f"Passed: {passed}")
    print(f"Failed: {failed}")
    print(f"Overall: {'PASS' if overall_pass else 'FAIL'}")
    print("Report written: reports/parseability_report.md")
    print("Inventory rebuilt: reports/package_inventory.yaml")
    print("Checkpoint updated: registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml")

    if overall_pass:
        print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CHECKSUMS")
        return 0

    print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-PARSEABILITY-FIX")
    return 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(check_package())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1")
CHECKSUM_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "checksums.sha256"
CHECKSUM_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "checksum_report.md"
INVENTORY_FILE = ROOT / "reports" / "package_inventory.yaml"
CHECKPOINT_FILE = ROOT / "registries" / "checkpoint_lock.yaml"
CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "candidate_export_status.yaml"

EXCLUDED_NAMES = {
    ".DS_Store",
    "Thumbs.db",
}

EXCLUDED_SUFFIXES = {
    ".tmp",
    ".bak",
    ".swp",
}

EXPECTED_FILES = [
    "manifest.yaml",
    "README.md",
    "protocol_core.md",
    "portable_ingestion_card.md",

    "schemas/source_registry.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/decision_log.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/verification_matrix.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/checkpoint_lock.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/command_palette.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/materialization_audit.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/portable_packet.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/role_binding.schema.yaml",
    "schemas/conflict_register.schema.yaml",

    "registries/source_registry.yaml",
    "registries/decision_log.yaml",
    "registries/verification_matrix.yaml",
    "registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml",
    "registries/command_palette.yaml",
    "registries/materialization_audit.yaml",
    "registries/role_binding_registry.yaml",
    "registries/conflict_register.yaml",
    "registries/next_action_menu.yaml",

    "evidence/raw_logs/README.raw_logs.md",
    "evidence/raw_logs/source_index.yaml",
    "evidence/metano_ia/README.metano_ia.md",
    "evidence/metano_ia/source_index.yaml",
    "evidence/branches/README.branches.md",
    "evidence/branches/branch_index.yaml",

    "reports/materialization_report.md",
    "reports/parseability_report.md",
    "reports/package_inventory.yaml",
    "reports/unresolved_gates.md",

    "release/checksums.sha256",
    "release/release_notes.md",
    "release/candidate_export_status.yaml",
]

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def load_yaml_module() -> Any:
    try:
        import yaml  # type: ignore
        return yaml
    except Exception:
        print("ERROR: PyYAML is required.")
        print("Install it with: python3 -m pip install pyyaml")
        sys.exit(2)

def should_include(path: Path) -> bool:
    if not path.is_file():
        return False
    if path.name in EXCLUDED_NAMES:
        return False
    if path.suffix in EXCLUDED_SUFFIXES:
        return False
    return True

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def assert_package_exists() -> None:
    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        print(f"FAIL: package root not found: {ROOT}")
        print("Run first:")
        print("  python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py")
        print("  python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py")
        sys.exit(1)

def assert_parseability_passed() -> None:
    if not (ROOT / "reports" / "parseability_report.md").exists():
        print("FAIL: reports/parseability_report.md not found.")
        print("Run first: python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    report = read_text(ROOT / "reports" / "parseability_report.md")
    if "Overall status: `PASS`" not in report:
        print("FAIL: parseability report does not show PASS.")
        print("Run/fix first: python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py")
        sys.exit(1)

def assert_expected_files_exist() -> list[str]:
    missing = []
    for relative_path in EXPECTED_FILES:
        path = ROOT / relative_path
        if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
            missing.append(relative_path)

    if missing:
        print("FAIL: missing expected files:")
        for item in missing:
            print(f"  - {item}")
        sys.exit(1)

    return missing

def collect_files() -> list[Path]:
    files = sorted(path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if should_include(path))

    # checksums.sha256 must be included in expected inventory but must not hash itself,
    # otherwise the checksum changes every time it is written.
    files = [path for path in files if path != CHECKSUM_FILE]

    return files

def write_checksums(files: list[Path]) -> dict[str, str]:
    checksum_map: dict[str, str] = {}

    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1 SHA-256 checksums",
        f"# generated_at: {now_iso()}",
        "# format: <sha256_hash>  <relative_file_path>",
    ]

    for path in files:
        relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        digest = sha256_file(path)
        checksum_map[relative] = digest
        lines.append(f"{digest}  {relative}")

    write_text(CHECKSUM_FILE, "\n".join(lines))
    return checksum_map

def update_inventory(yaml_module: Any, checksum_map: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    if not INVENTORY_FILE.exists():
        print("WARN: package_inventory.yaml not found; skipping inventory update.")
        return

    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(INVENTORY_FILE)) or {}
    inventory = data.setdefault("package_inventory", {})
    inventory["generated_at"] = now_iso()
    inventory["status"] = "checksum_signed"

    files = inventory.setdefault("files", [])
    for entry in files:
        relative = entry.get("path")
        if not relative:
            continue

        if relative == "release/checksums.sha256":
            entry["checksum"] = {
                "algorithm": "sha256",
                "value": "SELF_EXCLUDED_FROM_HASH_SET",
            }
            entry["promotion_status"] = "PROMOTABLE_TO_CANDIDATE_EXPORT"
            continue

        digest = checksum_map.get(relative)
        entry["checksum"] = {
            "algorithm": "sha256",
            "value": digest,
        }

        if digest:
            entry["promotion_status"] = "PROMOTABLE_TO_CANDIDATE_EXPORT"
        else:
            entry["promotion_status"] = "BLOCKED_MISSING_CHECKSUM"

    write_text(INVENTORY_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    if not CHECKPOINT_FILE.exists():
        print("WARN: checkpoint_lock.yaml not found; skipping checkpoint update.")
        return

    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE)) or {}
    lock = data.setdefault("checkpoint_lock", {})
    lock["current_phase"] = "PROMETHEUS.PHASE2.CHECKSUMS"
    lock["status"] = "ACTIVE"

    gates = lock.setdefault("gates", {})
    gates["standalone_files_materialized"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["yaml_parseability_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["markdown_package_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["checksums_generated"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["candidate_export"] = {"status": "READY_NEXT"}
    gates["final_commit"] = {"status": "BLOCKED"}
    gates["user_snapshot_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}
    gates["user_final_commit_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}

    lock["runtime_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["implementation_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["deployment_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["snapshot_approval"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["final_commit"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["next_safe_command"] = {
        "command": "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CANDIDATE-EXPORT",
        "reason": "Checksums generated. Candidate export may proceed if checkpoint lock remains blocked for runtime/implementation/final commit.",
    }

    write_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def update_candidate_status(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = {
        "candidate_export_status": {
            "package_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1",
            "status": "ready_for_candidate_export",
            "updated_at": now_iso(),
            "runtime_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "snapshot_approval": "BLOCKED",
            "final_commit": False,
            "ready_after": [
                "file_materialization_PASS",
                "parseability_PASS",
                "checksums_PASS",
            ],
            "next_safe_command": "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CANDIDATE-EXPORT",
            "blocked_commands": [
                "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
                "/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT",
                "/PROMETHEUS-RUNTIME-UNLOCK",
                "/PROMETHEUS-IMPLEMENTATION-UNLOCK",
            ],
        }
    }

    write_text(CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_checksum_report(files: list[Path], checksum_map: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    lines = [
        "# Checksum Report",
        "",
        f"Generated at: `{now_iso()}`",
        "",
        "## Status",
        "",
        "`PASS`",
        "",
        "## Summary",
        "",
        f"- Files hashed: {len(files)}",
        "- Algorithm: SHA-256",
        "- Output: `release/checksums.sha256`",
        "- `release/checksums.sha256` is excluded from its own hash set.",
        "",
        "## Truth Locks",
        "",
        "- runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- final_commit: BLOCKED",
        "",
        "## Next Safe Command",
        "",
        "```text",
        "/PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CANDIDATE-EXPORT",
        "```",
        "",
        "## Hash Inventory",
        "",
    ]

    for relative, digest in sorted(checksum_map.items()):
        lines.append(f"- `{relative}` — `{digest}`")

    write_text(CHECKSUM_REPORT, "\n".join(lines))

def validate_checksum_file(checksum_map: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    text = read_text(CHECKSUM_FILE)
    missing = []
    for relative, digest in checksum_map.items():
        expected_line = f"{digest}  {relative}"
        if expected_line not in text:
            missing.append(relative)

    if missing:
        print("FAIL: checksum file missing entries:")
        for item in missing:
            print(f"  - {item}")
        sys.exit(1)

def main() -> int:
    yaml_module = load_yaml_module()

    assert_package_exists()
    assert_parseability_passed()
    assert_expected_files_exist()

    files = collect_files()
    if not files:
        print("FAIL: no files collected for checksum.")
        return 1

    checksum_map = write_checksums(files)
    validate_checksum_file(checksum_map)

    update_inventory(yaml_module, checksum_map)
    update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module)
    update_candidate_status(yaml_module)
    write_checksum_report(files, checksum_map)

    print("PROMETHEUS checksum generation complete.")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Files hashed: {len(files)}")
    print(f"Checksum file: {CHECKSUM_FILE}")
    print(f"Checksum report: {CHECKSUM_REPORT}")
    print("Overall: PASS")
    print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-PHASE-2-CANDIDATE-EXPORT")
    print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, final_commit=BLOCKED")

    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys
import zipfile
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1")
ZIP_PATH = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip")
ZIP_SHA_PATH = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip.sha256")

CHECKSUM_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "checksums.sha256"
CHECKSUM_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "checksum_report.md"
PARSEABILITY_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "parseability_report.md"
CHECKPOINT_FILE = ROOT / "registries" / "checkpoint_lock.yaml"
INVENTORY_FILE = ROOT / "reports" / "package_inventory.yaml"
CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "candidate_export_status.yaml"
CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "candidate_package_manifest.yaml"
CANDIDATE_REPORT_FILE = ROOT / "reports" / "candidate_export_report.md"

EXCLUDED_NAMES = {
    ".DS_Store",
    "Thumbs.db",
}

EXCLUDED_SUFFIXES = {
    ".tmp",
    ".bak",
    ".swp",
}

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def load_yaml_module() -> Any:
    try:
        import yaml  # type: ignore
        return yaml
    except Exception:
        print("ERROR: PyYAML is required.")
        print("Install it with: python3 -m pip install pyyaml")
        sys.exit(2)

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def should_include(path: Path) -> bool:
    if not path.is_file():
        return False
    if path.name in EXCLUDED_NAMES:
        return False
    if path.suffix in EXCLUDED_SUFFIXES:
        return False
    return True

def collect_package_files(include_checksum_file: bool = True) -> list[Path]:
    files = sorted(path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if should_include(path))
    if not include_checksum_file:
        files = [path for path in files if path != CHECKSUM_FILE]
    return files

def assert_preconditions() -> None:
    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        print(f"FAIL: package root not found: {ROOT}")
        print("Run first: python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not PARSEABILITY_REPORT.exists():
        print("FAIL: parseability report missing.")
        print("Run first: python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    parse_report = read_text(PARSEABILITY_REPORT)
    if "Overall status: `PASS`" not in parse_report:
        print("FAIL: parseability report does not show PASS.")
        print("Run/fix first: python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not CHECKSUM_REPORT.exists():
        print("FAIL: checksum report missing.")
        print("Run first: python3 generate_prometheus_checksums.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    checksum_report = read_text(CHECKSUM_REPORT)
    if "`PASS`" not in checksum_report:
        print("FAIL: checksum report does not show PASS.")
        print("Run/fix first: python3 generate_prometheus_checksums.py")
        sys.exit(1)

def update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data: dict[str, Any]
    if CHECKPOINT_FILE.exists():
        data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE)) or {}
    else:
        data = {}

    lock = data.setdefault("checkpoint_lock", {})
    lock["lock_id"] = "LOCK.PROM.PHASE2.CANDIDATE_EXPORT.001"
    lock["version"] = "0.1"
    lock["current_phase"] = "PROMETHEUS.PHASE2.CANDIDATE_EXPORT"
    lock["status"] = "ACTIVE"

    lock["runtime_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["implementation_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["deployment_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["snapshot_approval"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["final_commit"] = "BLOCKED"

    gates = lock.setdefault("gates", {})
    gates["standalone_files_materialized"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["yaml_parseability_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["markdown_package_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["checksums_generated"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["candidate_export"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["snapshot_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}
    gates["final_commit"] = {"status": "BLOCKED"}
    gates["user_snapshot_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}
    gates["user_final_commit_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}

    lock["next_safe_command"] = {
        "command": "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
        "class": "gated",
        "approval_required": True,
        "reason": "Candidate package exported. Snapshot approval remains a user-gated step.",
    }

    write_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_candidate_status(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = {
        "candidate_export_status": {
            "package_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1",
            "candidate_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE",
            "status": "candidate_exported",
            "exported_at": now_iso(),
            "zip_file": ZIP_PATH.name,
            "zip_checksum_file": ZIP_SHA_PATH.name,
            "runtime_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth": "BLOCKED",
            "snapshot_approval": "BLOCKED",
            "final_commit": False,
            "passed_gates": [
                "file_materialization",
                "parseability",
                "checksums",
                "candidate_export",
            ],
            "blocked_gates": [
                "snapshot_approval",
                "final_commit",
                "runtime_truth",
                "implementation_truth",
                "deployment_truth",
            ],
            "next_safe_command": "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
        }
    }

    write_text(CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_candidate_manifest(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = {
        "candidate_package_manifest": {
            "candidate_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE",
            "package_root": ROOT.as_posix(),
            "zip_file": ZIP_PATH.name,
            "zip_checksum_file": ZIP_SHA_PATH.name,
            "created_at": now_iso(),
            "status": "candidate_exported_not_final_commit",
            "parent_system": "METANO_IA",
            "subsystem": "PROMETHEUS",
            "scope": [
                "documentation_workflow_binding_context",
                "source_governance",
                "decision_tracking",
                "verification_governance",
                "checkpoint_governance",
                "command_governance",
                "role_binding",
                "packetization",
                "rehydration_handoff",
            ],
            "truth_locks": {
                "runtime_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "implementation_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "deployment_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "snapshot_approval": "BLOCKED",
                "final_commit": "BLOCKED",
            },
            "included_roots": [
                "manifest.yaml",
                "README.md",
                "protocol_core.md",
                "portable_ingestion_card.md",
                "schemas/",
                "registries/",
                "evidence/",
                "reports/",
                "release/",
            ],
            "next_safe_command": "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
        }
    }

    write_text(CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_candidate_report() -> None:
    report = f"""
# Candidate Export Report

Generated at: `{now_iso()}`

## Status

`PASS`

## Candidate Package

- Package root: `{ROOT.as_posix()}`
- Candidate ZIP: `{ZIP_PATH.name}`
- Candidate ZIP checksum: `{ZIP_SHA_PATH.name}`

## Passed Gates

- File materialization: PASS
- Parseability: PASS
- Checksums: PASS
- Candidate export: PASS

## Still Blocked

- snapshot_approval: BLOCKED
- final_commit: BLOCKED
- runtime_truth: BLOCKED
- implementation_truth: BLOCKED
- deployment_truth: BLOCKED

## Meaning

This package is a candidate export. It is not a final commit, not a runtime release, not an implementation proof, and not deployment truth.

## Next Safe Command

```text
/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT
```
"""
    write_text(CANDIDATE_REPORT_FILE, report)

def rebuild_inventory(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    files = collect_package_files(include_checksum_file=True)

    data = {
        "package_inventory": {
            "inventory_id": "PROMETHEUS.PACKAGE_INVENTORY.001",
            "package_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1",
            "generated_at": now_iso(),
            "status": "candidate_exported",
            "total_files": len(files),
            "files": [],
        }
    }

    for path in files:
        relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        data["package_inventory"]["files"].append(
            {
                "path": relative,
                "required": True,
                "exists": True,
                "parse_required": relative.endswith((".yaml", ".yml")),
                "parse_status": "PASS" if relative.endswith((".yaml", ".yml")) else "NOT_APPLICABLE",
                "checksum_source": "release/checksums.sha256",
                "promotion_status": "INCLUDED_IN_CANDIDATE_EXPORT",
            }
        )

    write_text(INVENTORY_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def regenerate_checksums() -> dict[str, str]:
    files = collect_package_files(include_checksum_file=False)
    checksum_map: dict[str, str] = {}

    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1 candidate SHA-256 checksums",
        f"# generated_at: {now_iso()}",
        "# format: <sha256_hash>  <relative_file_path>",
        "# release/checksums.sha256 is excluded from its own hash set",
    ]

    for path in files:
        relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        digest = sha256_file(path)
        checksum_map[relative] = digest
        lines.append(f"{digest}  {relative}")

    write_text(CHECKSUM_FILE, "\n".join(lines))
    return checksum_map

def verify_checksums(checksum_map: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    text = read_text(CHECKSUM_FILE)
    missing = []

    for relative, digest in checksum_map.items():
        expected = f"{digest}  {relative}"
        if expected not in text:
            missing.append(relative)

    if missing:
        print("FAIL: checksum file missing entries after regeneration:")
        for item in missing:
            print(f"  - {item}")
        sys.exit(1)

def create_zip() -> None:
    if ZIP_PATH.exists():
        ZIP_PATH.unlink()

    files = collect_package_files(include_checksum_file=True)

    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, mode="w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        for path in files:
            archive_name = Path(ROOT.name) / path.relative_to(ROOT)
            archive.write(path, archive_name.as_posix())

def write_zip_checksum() -> str:
    digest = sha256_file(ZIP_PATH)
    content = f"{digest}  {ZIP_PATH.name}"
    write_text(ZIP_SHA_PATH, content)
    return digest

def validate_zip() -> int:
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, mode="r") as archive:
        bad = archive.testzip()
        if bad is not None:
            print(f"FAIL: corrupted file in ZIP: {bad}")
            return 1

        names = archive.namelist()
        required = [
            f"{ROOT.name}/manifest.yaml",
            f"{ROOT.name}/README.md",
            f"{ROOT.name}/registries/checkpoint_lock.yaml",
            f"{ROOT.name}/release/checksums.sha256",
            f"{ROOT.name}/reports/candidate_export_report.md",
            f"{ROOT.name}/release/candidate_package_manifest.yaml",
        ]

        missing = [item for item in required if item not in names]
        if missing:
            print("FAIL: required files missing from ZIP:")
            for item in missing:
                print(f"  - {item}")
            return 1

    return 0

def main() -> int:
    yaml_module = load_yaml_module()

    assert_preconditions()

    update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module)
    write_candidate_status(yaml_module)
    write_candidate_manifest(yaml_module)
    write_candidate_report()
    rebuild_inventory(yaml_module)

    checksum_map = regenerate_checksums()
    verify_checksums(checksum_map)

    create_zip()
    zip_digest = write_zip_checksum()

    zip_validation = validate_zip()
    if zip_validation != 0:
        return zip_validation

    files_in_package = collect_package_files(include_checksum_file=True)

    print("PROMETHEUS candidate export complete.")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Candidate ZIP: {ZIP_PATH.resolve()}")
    print(f"ZIP SHA-256: {zip_digest}")
    print(f"ZIP checksum file: {ZIP_SHA_PATH.resolve()}")
    print(f"Files included from package root: {len(files_in_package)}")
    print("Overall: PASS")
    print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT")
    print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, final_commit=BLOCKED")

    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python id="nz5qdc"
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys
import zipfile
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1")
CANDIDATE_ZIP = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip")
CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA = Path("PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip.sha256")

CHECKPOINT_FILE = ROOT / "registries" / "checkpoint_lock.yaml"
CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "candidate_export_status.yaml"
SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "snapshot_approval_record.yaml"
SNAPSHOT_REPORT_FILE = ROOT / "reports" / "snapshot_approval_report.md"
CHECKSUM_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "checksums.sha256"
INVENTORY_FILE = ROOT / "reports" / "package_inventory.yaml"

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def load_yaml_module() -> Any:
    try:
        import yaml  # type: ignore
        return yaml
    except Exception:
        print("ERROR: PyYAML is required.")
        print("Install it with: python3 -m pip install pyyaml")
        sys.exit(2)

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def assert_candidate_export_passed() -> None:
    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        print(f"FAIL: package root not found: {ROOT}")
        print("Run first: python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not CANDIDATE_ZIP.exists():
        print(f"FAIL: candidate ZIP not found: {CANDIDATE_ZIP}")
        print("Run first: python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA.exists():
        print(f"FAIL: candidate ZIP checksum file not found: {CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA}")
        print("Run first: python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    if not CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE.exists():
        print(f"FAIL: candidate export status not found: {CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE}")
        print("Run first: python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    status_text = read_text(CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE)
    if "candidate_exported" not in status_text:
        print("FAIL: candidate_export_status.yaml does not show candidate_exported.")
        print("Run/fix first: python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py")
        sys.exit(1)

    with zipfile.ZipFile(CANDIDATE_ZIP, mode="r") as archive:
        bad_file = archive.testzip()
        if bad_file is not None:
            print(f"FAIL: corrupted file in candidate ZIP: {bad_file}")
            sys.exit(1)

    actual_digest = sha256_file(CANDIDATE_ZIP)
    expected_text = read_text(CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA)

    if actual_digest not in expected_text:
        print("FAIL: candidate ZIP checksum mismatch.")
        print(f"Expected file content: {CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA}")
        print(f"Actual digest: {actual_digest}")
        sys.exit(1)

def assert_truth_locks_safe(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    if not CHECKPOINT_FILE.exists():
        print(f"FAIL: checkpoint lock missing: {CHECKPOINT_FILE}")
        sys.exit(1)

    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE)) or {}
    lock = data.get("checkpoint_lock", {})

    runtime_truth = lock.get("runtime_truth")
    implementation_truth = lock.get("implementation_truth")
    final_commit = lock.get("final_commit")

    unsafe = []

    if runtime_truth != "BLOCKED":
        unsafe.append(f"runtime_truth={runtime_truth}")

    if implementation_truth != "BLOCKED":
        unsafe.append(f"implementation_truth={implementation_truth}")

    if final_commit not in {"BLOCKED", False}:
        unsafe.append(f"final_commit={final_commit}")

    if unsafe:
        print("FAIL: unsafe truth lock state detected:")
        for item in unsafe:
            print(f"  - {item}")
        print("Snapshot approval cannot proceed.")
        sys.exit(1)

def update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE)) or {}
    lock = data.setdefault("checkpoint_lock", {})

    lock["lock_id"] = "LOCK.PROM.SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL.001"
    lock["current_phase"] = "PROMETHEUS.SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL"
    lock["status"] = "ACTIVE"

    lock["runtime_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["implementation_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["deployment_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    lock["snapshot_approval"] = "APPROVED"
    lock["final_commit"] = "BLOCKED"

    gates = lock.setdefault("gates", {})
    gates["standalone_files_materialized"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["yaml_parseability_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["markdown_package_verified"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["checksums_generated"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["candidate_export"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["snapshot_approval"] = {"status": "PASS"}
    gates["user_snapshot_approval"] = {
        "status": "PASS",
        "approval_source": "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
        "approved_at": now_iso(),
    }
    gates["final_commit"] = {"status": "READY_NEXT"}
    gates["user_final_commit_approval"] = {"status": "NOT_REQUESTED"}

    lock["next_safe_command"] = {
        "command": "/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT",
        "class": "gated",
        "approval_required": True,
        "reason": "Snapshot approved. Final commit/export remains a separate gated command.",
    }

    write_text(CHECKPOINT_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_snapshot_approval_record(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = {
        "snapshot_approval_record": {
            "approval_id": "APPROVAL.PROM.SNAPSHOT.001",
            "package_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1",
            "candidate_id": "PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE",
            "approved_at": now_iso(),
            "approval_command": "/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT",
            "approved_by": {
                "name": "Raquel Alejandra",
                "role": "concept_owner_content_owner_final_decision_authority",
            },
            "approval_scope": [
                "documentation_workflow_binding_context",
                "source_governance",
                "decision_tracking",
                "verification_governance",
                "checkpoint_governance",
                "command_governance",
                "role_binding",
                "packetization",
                "rehydration_handoff",
            ],
            "explicit_non_approval": [
                "runtime_truth",
                "implementation_truth",
                "deployment_truth",
                "source_code_behavior_truth",
                "automatic_final_commit",
            ],
            "truth_locks_after_approval": {
                "runtime_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "implementation_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "deployment_truth": "BLOCKED",
                "snapshot_approval": "APPROVED",
                "final_commit": "BLOCKED",
            },
            "next_safe_command": "/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT",
        }
    }

    write_text(SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def update_candidate_status(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE)) or {}
    status = data.setdefault("candidate_export_status", {})

    status["snapshot_approval"] = "APPROVED"
    status["snapshot_approved_at"] = now_iso()
    status["final_commit"] = False
    status["runtime_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    status["implementation_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    status["deployment_truth"] = "BLOCKED"
    status["next_safe_command"] = "/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT"

    write_text(CANDIDATE_STATUS_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def write_snapshot_report() -> None:
    report = f"""
# Snapshot Approval Report

Generated at: `{now_iso()}`

## Status

`SNAPSHOT_APPROVED`

## Approved Package

- Package root: `{ROOT.as_posix()}`
- Candidate ZIP: `{CANDIDATE_ZIP.name}`
- Candidate ZIP checksum file: `{CANDIDATE_ZIP_SHA.name}`

## Approval Source

```text
/PROMETHEUS-APPROVE-SNAPSHOT
```

## Approved Scope

- documentation_workflow_binding_context
- source_governance
- decision_tracking
- verification_governance
- checkpoint_governance
- command_governance
- role_binding
- packetization
- rehydration_handoff

## Explicitly Not Approved

- runtime_truth
- implementation_truth
- deployment_truth
- source_code_behavior_truth
- automatic_final_commit

## Truth Locks After Approval

- runtime_truth: BLOCKED
- implementation_truth: BLOCKED
- deployment_truth: BLOCKED
- snapshot_approval: APPROVED
- final_commit: BLOCKED

## Next Safe Command

```text
/PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT
```
"""
    write_text(SNAPSHOT_REPORT_FILE, report)

def regenerate_package_checksums() -> None:
    files = sorted(
        path for path in ROOT.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file()
        and path.name not in {".DS_Store", "Thumbs.db"}
        and path.suffix not in {".tmp", ".bak", ".swp"}
        and path != CHECKSUM_FILE
    )

    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1 snapshot-approved SHA-256 checksums",
        f"# generated_at: {now_iso()}",
        "# format: <sha256_hash>  <relative_file_path>",
        "# release/checksums.sha256 is excluded from its own hash set",
    ]

    for path in files:
        relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        digest = sha256_file(path)
        lines.append(f"{digest}  {relative}")

    write_text(CHECKSUM_FILE, "\n".join(lines))

def update_inventory(yaml_module: Any) -> None:
    if not INVENTORY_FILE.exists():
        return

    data = yaml_module.safe_load(read_text(INVENTORY_FILE)) or {}
    inventory = data.setdefault("package_inventory", {})
    inventory["generated_at"] = now_iso()
    inventory["status"] = "snapshot_approved"

    files = inventory.setdefault("files", [])
    existing_paths = {entry.get("path") for entry in files}

    for required_path in [
        "release/snapshot_approval_record.yaml",
        "reports/snapshot_approval_report.md",
    ]:
        if required_path not in existing_paths:
            files.append(
                {
                    "path": required_path,
                    "required": True,
                    "exists": True,
                    "parse_required": required_path.endswith(".yaml"),
                    "parse_status": "PASS" if required_path.endswith(".yaml") else "NOT_APPLICABLE",
                    "checksum_source": "release/checksums.sha256",
                    "promotion_status": "INCLUDED_IN_SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL",
                }
            )

    write_text(INVENTORY_FILE, yaml_module.safe_dump(data, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True))

def main() -> int:
    yaml_module = load_yaml_module()

    assert_candidate_export_passed()
    assert_truth_locks_safe(yaml_module)

    write_snapshot_approval_record(yaml_module)
    update_checkpoint_lock(yaml_module)
    update_candidate_status(yaml_module)
    write_snapshot_report()
    update_inventory(yaml_module)
    regenerate_package_checksums()

    print("PROMETHEUS snapshot approval complete.")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Approval record: {SNAPSHOT_APPROVAL_FILE}")
    print(f"Approval report: {SNAPSHOT_REPORT_FILE}")
    print("Overall: PASS")
    print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-COMMIT-AND-EXPORT")
    print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, final_commit=BLOCKED")

    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

# PROMETHEUS_POSTCOMMIT_RUNBOOK.md

```markdown
# PROMETHEUS Post-Commit Runbook

**System:** PROMETHEUS  
**Parent system:** METANO_IA  
**Artifact:** PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1  
**Status:** FINAL_COMMIT_APPROVED_FOR_DOCUMENTATION_WORKFLOW_BINDING_ONLY  
**Runtime truth:** BLOCKED  
**Implementation truth:** BLOCKED  
**Deployment truth:** BLOCKED  

## 1. Purpose

This runbook explains how to use, reconstruct, validate, rehydrate, and integrate PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1 after final approval for documentation/workflow/binding.

PROMETHEUS is approved as a governance, preservation, verification, packetization, and rehydration subsystem.

It is not approved as runtime, implementation, deployment, or source-code truth.

## 2. Approved Scope

Approved:

- documentation_workflow_binding_context
- source_governance
- decision_tracking
- verification_governance
- checkpoint_governance
- command_governance
- role_binding
- packetization
- rehydration_handoff

Not approved:

- runtime_truth
- implementation_truth
- deployment_truth
- source_code_behavior_truth
- automatic_runtime_claims
- automatic_implementation_claims

## 3. Operating Boundary

METANO_IA is the parent orchestration ecosystem.

PROMETHEUS is a subsystem inside METANO_IA.

METANO_IA governs:

- project continuity
- role division
- memory regeneration
- non-destructive workflow
- iterative execution

PROMETHEUS governs:

- source registry
- truth hierarchy
- verification matrix
- checkpoint lock
- materialization audit
- command palette
- role binding
- rescue before delete
- portable packet ingestion

## 4. Final Approval Statement

```text
FINAL_COMMIT_APPROVED_FOR_DOCUMENTATION_WORKFLOW_BINDING_ONLY
```

This approval does not unlock runtime, implementation, or deployment.

## 5. Local Execution Order

Run these in order:

```bash
python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py
python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py
python3 generate_prometheus_checksums.py
python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py
python3 approve_prometheus_snapshot.py
```

Optional final packaging step:

```bash
python3 export_prometheus_final.py
```

If the final export script does not exist yet, create it from the final commit/export specification before running.

## 6. Expected Local Artifacts

After successful execution, the working folder should include:

```text
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1/
├── manifest.yaml
├── README.md
├── protocol_core.md
├── portable_ingestion_card.md
├── schemas/
├── registries/
├── evidence/
├── reports/
└── release/
```

Expected candidate package:

```text
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_CANDIDATE.zip.sha256
```

Expected approval files:

```text
release/snapshot_approval_record.yaml
reports/snapshot_approval_report.md
```

Expected final files, after final export:

```text
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_FINAL.zip
PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE_v0.1_FINAL.zip.sha256
release/final_commit_record.yaml
release/final_export_manifest.yaml
release/final_release_notes.md
release/final_checksums.sha256
```

## 7. Pass Criteria

A valid candidate package requires:

- package folder exists
- all expected files exist
- YAML files parse
- Markdown files are non-empty
- package inventory exists
- checksums exist
- candidate ZIP exists
- candidate ZIP validates
- checkpoint lock keeps runtime truth BLOCKED
- checkpoint lock keeps implementation truth BLOCKED
- final commit remains scoped to documentation/workflow/binding only

## 8. Fail Criteria

Block promotion if any of these occurs:

- missing package root
- missing required file
- YAML parse failure
- empty required Markdown
- missing checksum
- checksum mismatch
- corrupted ZIP
- runtime_truth marked VERIFIED without source evidence
- implementation_truth marked VERIFIED without source evidence
- final commit claims runtime or deployment

## 9. Recovery Matrix

### Missing package folder

Run:

```bash
python3 materialize_prometheus_protocol_core.py
```

Then rerun:

```bash
python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py
```

### YAML parse failure

Open:

```text
reports/parseability_report.md
```

Fix the listed YAML file. Then rerun:

```bash
python3 check_prometheus_parseability.py
```

### Missing checksum

Run:

```bash
python3 generate_prometheus_checksums.py
```

### Candidate ZIP missing

Run:

```bash
python3 export_prometheus_candidate.py
```

### Snapshot approval missing

Run:

```bash
python3 approve_prometheus_snapshot.py
```

### Runtime or implementation accidentally unlocked

Stop.

Restore checkpoint lock:

```yaml
runtime_truth: BLOCKED
implementation_truth: BLOCKED
deployment_truth: BLOCKED
final_commit: APPROVED_FOR_DOCUMENTATION_WORKFLOW_BINDING_ONLY
```

Then rerun parseability and checksums.

## 10. Rehydration Use

Use `portable_ingestion_card.md` to install PROMETHEUS into a new chat or model.

Minimum rehydration instruction:

```text
REHYDRATE | SYSTEM="PROMETHEUS" | PROTOCOL="PROMETHEUS_PROTOCOL_CORE" | VERSION="0.1" | PARENT_SYSTEM="METANO_IA" | MODE="PORTABLE_PACKET_INGESTION"

Install PROMETHEUS as a governance, preservation, verification and packetization subsystem.

Do not treat this packet as runtime truth, implementation truth, deployment truth, source-code truth, final commit approval or automatic snapshot approval.
```

Expected first response:

```yaml
rehydration_report:
  system: PROMETHEUS
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  protocol_version: 0.1
  active_scope:
    - documentation_workflow_binding_context
    - source_registry
    - decision_log
    - verification_matrix
    - checkpoint_lock
    - command_palette
    - role_binding
    - packetization
    - rehydration_handoff
  blocked_scope:
    - runtime_truth
    - implementation_truth
    - deployment_truth
    - source_code_behavior_truth
  checkpoint_lock:
    runtime_truth: BLOCKED
    implementation_truth: BLOCKED
    deployment_truth: BLOCKED
    final_commit: APPROVED_FOR_DOCUMENTATION_WORKFLOW_BINDING_ONLY
  next_action_menu:
    - /inventory
    - /classify
    - /verification-matrix
    - /checkpoint-lock
```

## 11. METANO_IA Handoff

Register PROMETHEUS inside METANO_IA as:

```yaml
METANO_IA:
  subsystems:
    PROMETHEUS:
      type: governance_preservation_verification_packetization_subsystem
      status: committed_docs_workflow_binding
      approved_scope:
        - source_registry
        - truth_hierarchy
        - verification_matrix
        - checkpoint_lock
        - materialization_audit
        - command_palette

        - packetization
        - rehydration_handoff
      blocked_scope:
        - runtime_truth
        - implementation_truth
        - deployment_truth
      activation_command: /rehydrate-prometheus
```

```

## 13. Operator Rules

Never claim:

```yaml
forbidden_claims:
  - runtime_ready
  - implementation_ready
  - deployed
  - source_code_verified
  - production_ready
```

Allowed claim:

  - PROMETHEUS Protocol Core v0.1 is final-committed for documentation/workflow/binding governance only.

# 3. Unified packet body

```yaml id="unified-packet-body"
unified_packet:
  purpose: >
    Rehidratar Prometheus como subsistema portable de preservación,
    verificación, gobernanza, packetización y continuidad operativa.

    system: PROMETHEUS

    parent: METANO_IA

  operator_rule:
    Raquel:
      authority:
        - concept
        - content
        - criteria
        - final_decision
        - approval
    assistant:
      authority:
        - structure
        - architecture
        - validation
        - normalization
        - technical_execution
      cannot:
        - approve_final_commit_without_user
        - unlock_runtime_truth_without_source_evidence
        - unlock_implementation_truth_without_source_evidence
```

---

# Role boundary

```yaml id="role-boundary-metanoia-prometheus"
role_boundary:
  Raquel:
    role:
      - concept_owner
      - content_owner
      - criteria_owner
      - final_decision_authority
      - approval_authority

    can:
      - approve_snapshot
      - approve_commit
      - override_direction
      - decide_project_scope
      - accept_or_reject_protocol_promotion

  assistant:
    role:
      - technical_architect
      - structure_operator
      - validation_operator
      - normalization_operator
      - documentation_builder
      - protocol_drafter

    can:
      - organize
      - classify
      - verify
      - draft
      - generate_structures
      - propose_next_actions
      - maintain_truth_locks

    cannot:
      - approve_final_commit_without_Raquel
      - unlock_runtime_truth_without_source_evidence
      - unlock_implementation_truth_without_source_evidence
      - claim_deployment_truth_without_evidence
      - delete_or_suppress_branch_lineage
      - merge_unrelated_contexts_without_permission

  PROMETHEUS:
    role:
      - truth_guardian
      - packetization_guardian
      - checkpoint_guardian
      - source_registry_guardian
      - rehydration_guardian

    can:
      - classify_sources
      - preserve_lineage
      - block_false_promotion
      - generate_packet_structure
      - produce_handoff_context
      - maintain_rehydration_cards

    cannot:
      - replace_METANO_IA
      - override_Raquel
      - become_runtime_engine_by_claim
      - become_implementation_truth_by_claim
      - promote_AI_output_to_canon_without_verification
```

---

# Non-use policy

```yaml id="prometheus-non-use-policy"
non_use_policy:
  PROMETHEUS_must_not_be_used_as:
    - replacement_for_METANO_IA
    - proof_of_runtime
    - proof_of_implementation
    - proof_of_deployment
    - source_code_truth
    - automatic_commit_authority
    - universal_context_merger_without_scope

  PROMETHEUS_must_be_used_as:
    - preservation_subsystem
    - verification_subsystem
    - packetization_subsystem
    - source_governance_subsystem
    - checkpoint_lock_subsystem
    - handoff_subsystem
```

Load PROMETHEUS as an active subsystem of METANO_IA.

PROMETHEUS handles:
- source governance
- truth hierarchy
- verification matrix
- checkpoint locks

- packetization
- rehydration handoff

PROMETHEUS does not handle:

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import textwrap

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")

def clean(value: str) -> str:
    return textwrap.dedent(value).strip() + "\n"

def write_file(relative_path: str, content: str) -> None:
    path = ROOT / relative_path
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(clean(content), encoding="utf-8")

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

## Prompt:
23/6/2026, 9:39:54 p.m.

el formato por qué se descuadró?

## Response:
23/6/2026, 9:40:05 p.m.

Se descuadró por una razón muy concreta: **metí bloques Markdown con triple backtick dentro de un bloque `python` que también estaba envuelto con triple backtick**.

Ejemplo del problema:

````markdown
```python
files["README.md"] = """
```text
/PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-PARSEABILITY-CHECK
```
"""
```
````

El visor interpreta el primer:

```text
```
```

como cierre del bloque externo, aunque en realidad estaba dentro de un string de Python. Entonces el resto del script deja de renderizar como código y se rompe visualmente.

# Dictamen

```yaml
format_issue:
  type: markdown_fence_collision
  cause:
    - outer_code_block_used_triple_backticks
    - inner_generated_files_also_contained_triple_backticks
    - renderer_closed_code_block_early
  logic_status: not_invalidated
  affected:
    - visual_format
    - copy_paste_safety
  not_affected:
    - architectural_sequence
    - countdown
    - truth_locks
    - command_logic
```

# Cómo corregirlo

De aquí en adelante, para scripts que generan Markdown, usaré una de estas dos estrategias:

## Opción A — bloque externo con 4 backticks

`````markdown
````python
files["README.md"] = """
```text
safe inner block
```
"""
````
`````

## Opción B — no usar triple backticks dentro de strings generados

En vez de:

```python
"""
```text
/comando
```
"""
```

usar:

```python
"""
Command:

    /comando
"""
```

# Regla para PROMETHEUS

```yaml
format_guardrail:
  when_generating_python_that_writes_markdown:
    outer_fence: four_backticks
    inner_markdown_fences: allowed_but_controlled
    safer_default: avoid_nested_triple_backticks
```

# Veredicto

No fue un fallo del protocolo.  
Fue un **choque de formato Markdown por fences anidados**.

Para el siguiente movimiento, conviene rehacer ese script en formato limpio:

```text
/PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-FILE-MATERIALIZE-FORMAT-FIX
```

> **Corrigiendo y simplificando el script**
>
> Puedo dar una respuesta clara sin necesidad de herramientas externas. El código debe aparecer con formato limpio usando 4 backticks, sin contenedores innecesarios. Evitaré mencionar la creación de archivos, ya que no estamos interactuando con el sistema de archivos. También puedo enfocarme en una solución más directa y robusta, en español.
>
> **Generando script completo y corregido**
>
> Voy a proporcionar un código completo, evitando contenedores innecesarios. Incluiré la estructura adecuada en 4 backticks y me aseguraré de que el YAML esté bien indentado sin depender de PyYAML. El paquete debe generarse correctamente, y me aseguraré de que todos los archivos estén incluidos y sean válidos.

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import textwrap

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")

def clean(value: str) -> str:
    return textwrap.dedent(value).strip() + "\n"

def write_file(relative_path: str, content: str) -> None:
    path = ROOT / relative_path
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(clean(content), encoding="utf-8")

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def main() -> None:
    generated_at = now_iso()
    ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        rc_artifacts:
          - MANIFEST.PROMETHEUS.UNIFIED.v1.4.0-rc1.yaml

          - PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1_CANDIDATE_EXPORT

      approved_scope:

        v0_1_final_commit: APPROVED_FOR_DOCUMENTATION_WORKFLOW_BINDING_ONLY

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import textwrap

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")

def clean(value: str) -> str:
    return textwrap.dedent(value).strip() + "\n"

def write_file(relative_path: str, content: str) -> None:
    path = ROOT / relative_path
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(clean(content), encoding="utf-8")

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def main() -> None:
    generated_at = now_iso()
    ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

          - METANOIA_PROMETHEUS_INTEGRATION_BLOCK.yaml

    materialized = sorted(path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if path.is_file())

    print("PROMETHEUS v1.4.0-rc1 filesystem materialization complete.")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Files written: {len(materialized)}")
    print("Expected files: 22")

    print("Final commit as v1.4.0: NOT_REQUESTED")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

````python id="fence-safe-parseability-script"
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import sys

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")

EXPECTED_FILES = [
    "manifest.yaml",
    "README.md",
    "unified_packet.md",
    "rehydration_card.md",
    "CHANGELOG.md",
    "core/CORE_REFERENCE.md",
    "core/source_registry.yaml",
    "core/decision_log.yaml",
    "core/verification_matrix.yaml",
    "core/checkpoint_lock.yaml",
    "core/command_palette.yaml",
    "core/role_binding_registry.yaml",
    "core/conflict_register.yaml",
    "metanoia/METANOIA_PROMETHEUS_INTEGRATION_BLOCK.yaml",
    "reports/rehydration_test_report.md",
    "reports/materialization_report.md",
    "reports/format_fix_report.md",
    "reports/unresolved_gates.md",
    "reports/package_inventory.yaml",
    "release/candidate_export_record.yaml",
    "release/release_notes.md",
    "release/checksums.sha256",
]

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.strip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def try_import_yaml():
    try:
        import yaml
        return yaml, None
    except Exception as exc:
        return None, exc

def validate_required_files() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    results = []

    for relative in EXPECTED_FILES:
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_MISSING",
                "detail": "Required file does not exist.",
            })
            continue

        if not path.is_file():
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_NOT_FILE",
                "detail": "Path exists but is not a file.",
            })
            continue

        try:
            content = read_text(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_READ_ERROR",
                "detail": str(exc),
            })
            continue

        if not content.strip():
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_EMPTY",
                "detail": "File is empty.",
            })
            continue

        results.append({
            "path": relative,
            "status": "PASS_EXISTS_NONEMPTY",
            "detail": "File exists and is non-empty.",
        })

    return results

def validate_yaml_files() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    yaml_module, import_error = try_import_yaml()
    yaml_files = [p for p in EXPECTED_FILES if p.endswith(".yaml")]
    results = []

    if yaml_module is None:
        for relative in yaml_files:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_YAML_PARSER_MISSING",
                "detail": f"PyYAML is required. Install with: python3 -m pip install pyyaml. Error: {import_error}",
            })
        return results

    for relative in yaml_files:
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_MISSING",
                "detail": "Cannot parse because file is missing.",
            })
            continue

        try:
            content = read_text(path)
            parsed = yaml_module.safe_load(content)
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_YAML_PARSE",
                "detail": str(exc),
            })
            continue

        if parsed is None:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_YAML_EMPTY_DOCUMENT",
                "detail": "YAML parsed as empty document.",
            })
            continue

        results.append({
            "path": relative,
            "status": "PASS_YAML_PARSE",
            "detail": "YAML parsed successfully.",
        })

    return results

def validate_truth_locks() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    checks = [
        ("manifest.yaml", "runtime_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("manifest.yaml", "implementation_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("manifest.yaml", "deployment_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("manifest.yaml", "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED"),
        ("core/checkpoint_lock.yaml", "runtime_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("core/checkpoint_lock.yaml", "implementation_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("core/checkpoint_lock.yaml", "deployment_truth: BLOCKED"),
        ("core/checkpoint_lock.yaml", "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED"),
        ("release/candidate_export_record.yaml", "final_commit: false"),
    ]

    results = []

    for relative, required_text in checks:
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_MISSING",
                "detail": f"Cannot verify lock: {required_text}",
            })
            continue

        content = read_text(path)

        if required_text in content:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "PASS_LOCK_PRESENT",
                "detail": required_text,
            })
        else:
            results.append({
                "path": relative,
                "status": "FAIL_LOCK_MISSING",
                "detail": required_text,
            })

    return results

def update_checkpoint_after_pass(overall_status: str) -> None:
    checkpoint_path = ROOT / "core/checkpoint_lock.yaml"

    if not checkpoint_path.exists():
        return

    content = read_text(checkpoint_path)

    old_line = "parseability_check:\n          status: NOT_STARTED"
    new_line = f"parseability_check:\n          status: {overall_status}"

    if old_line in content:
        content = content.replace(old_line, new_line)

    next_old = "command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-PARSEABILITY-CHECK"
    next_new = "command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-CHECKSUMS"

    if overall_status == "PASS" and next_old in content:
        content = content.replace(next_old, next_new)

    write_text(checkpoint_path, content)

def make_report(
    file_results: list[dict[str, str]],
    yaml_results: list[dict[str, str]],
    lock_results: list[dict[str, str]],
    overall_status: str,
) -> str:
    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS v1.4.0-rc1 Parseability Report",
        "",
        f"Generated at: {now_iso()}",
        f"Package root: {ROOT}",
        f"Overall status: {overall_status}",
        "",
        "## File existence and non-empty checks",
        "",
    ]

    for result in file_results:
        lines.append(f"- {result['status']}: {result['path']} — {result['detail']}")

    lines.extend(["", "## YAML parse checks", ""])

    for result in yaml_results:
        lines.append(f"- {result['status']}: {result['path']} — {result['detail']}")

    lines.extend(["", "## Truth lock checks", ""])

    for result in lock_results:
        lines.append(f"- {result['status']}: {result['path']} — {result['detail']}")

    lines.extend([
        "",
        "## Locked boundaries",
        "",
        "- runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- final_commit_as_v1_4_0: NOT_REQUESTED",
        "",
        "## Next command",
        "",
    ])

    if overall_status == "PASS":
        lines.append("    /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-CHECKSUMS")
    else:
        lines.append("    Fix listed failures, then rerun /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-PARSEABILITY-CHECK")

    return "\n".join(lines)

def main() -> int:
    if not ROOT.exists():
        print("Overall: FAIL_PACKAGE_ROOT_MISSING")
        print(f"Missing package root: {ROOT}")
        print("Run first: python3 materialize_prometheus_v1_4_0_rc1.py")
        return 1

    file_results = validate_required_files()
    yaml_results = validate_yaml_files()
    lock_results = validate_truth_locks()

    all_results = file_results + yaml_results + lock_results
    failed = [item for item in all_results if item["status"].startswith("FAIL")]

    overall_status = "PASS" if not failed else "FAIL"

    report = make_report(
        file_results=file_results,
        yaml_results=yaml_results,
        lock_results=lock_results,
        overall_status=overall_status,
    )

    write_text(ROOT / "reports/parseability_report.md", report)

    if overall_status == "PASS":
        update_checkpoint_after_pass("PASS")

    print(f"Overall: {overall_status}")
    print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"Expected files checked: {len(EXPECTED_FILES)}")
    print(f"Failures: {len(failed)}")
    print(f"Report written: {(ROOT / 'reports/parseability_report.md').resolve()}")
    print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, deployment_truth=BLOCKED")
    print("Final commit as v1.4.0: NOT_REQUESTED")

    if overall_status == "PASS":
        print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-CHECKSUMS")
        return 0

    print("Next action: fix failures and rerun parseability check")
    return 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
````
