````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")
CHECKSUM_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "checksums.sha256"
CHECKSUM_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "checksum_report.md"
CHECKSUM_MANIFEST = ROOT / "release" / "checksum_manifest.yaml"
PARSEABILITY_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "parseability_report.md"
CHECKPOINT = ROOT / "core" / "checkpoint_lock.yaml"

EXCLUDED_FROM_HASH = {
    "release/checksums.sha256",
    "release/checksum_manifest.yaml",
    "reports/checksum_report.md",
}

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.strip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def relative_posix(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def assert_package_exists() -> None:
    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Package root missing. Run materialize_prometheus_v1_4_0_rc1.py first."
        )

def assert_parseability_passed() -> None:
    if not PARSEABILITY_REPORT.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Parseability report missing. Run check_prometheus_v1_4_0_rc1_parseability.py first."
        )

    content = read_text(PARSEABILITY_REPORT)

    if "Overall status: PASS" not in content:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Parseability has not passed. Fix parseability failures before checksums."
        )

def assert_truth_locks() -> None:
    required_checks = {
        "manifest.yaml": [
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED",
        ],
        "core/checkpoint_lock.yaml": [
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED",
        ],
        "release/candidate_export_record.yaml": [
            "final_commit: false",
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        ],
    }

    failures: list[str] = []

    for relative, required_terms in required_checks.items():
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            failures.append(f"{relative}: missing file")
            continue

        content = read_text(path)

        for term in required_terms:
            if term not in content:
                failures.append(f"{relative}: missing lock '{term}'")

    if failures:
        raise RuntimeError("Truth lock validation failed: " + "; ".join(failures))

def collect_files() -> list[Path]:
    files = []

    for path in sorted(ROOT.rglob("*")):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue

        relative = relative_posix(path)

        if relative in EXCLUDED_FROM_HASH:
            continue

        files.append(path)

    return files

def write_checksums(files: list[Path]) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    rows: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    for path in files:
        relative = relative_posix(path)
        digest = sha256_file(path)
        rows.append((digest, relative))

    checksum_lines = [f"{digest}  {relative}" for digest, relative in rows]
    write_text(CHECKSUM_FILE, "\n".join(checksum_lines))

    return rows

def verify_checksums(rows: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:
    failures: list[str] = []

    for expected_digest, relative in rows:
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            failures.append(f"{relative}: missing during verification")
            continue

        actual_digest = sha256_file(path)

        if actual_digest != expected_digest:
            failures.append(f"{relative}: checksum mismatch")

    return failures

def update_checkpoint() -> None:
    if not CHECKPOINT.exists():
        return

    content = read_text(CHECKPOINT)

    replacements = {
        "checksums:\n          status: NOT_STARTED": "checksums:\n          status: PASS",
        "command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-CHECKSUMS": "command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-ZIP-EXPORT",
    }

    for old, new in replacements.items():
        content = content.replace(old, new)

    write_text(CHECKPOINT, content)

def write_manifest(rows: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
    lines = [
        "checksum_manifest:",
        "  manifest_id: PROMETHEUS.v1.4.0-rc1.CHECKSUM_MANIFEST.001",
        "  packet_id: PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1",
        f'  generated_at: "{now_iso()}"',
        "  status: PASS",
        "  algorithm: sha256",
        f"  hashed_files: {len(rows)}",
        "  excluded_from_hash:",
    ]

    for item in sorted(EXCLUDED_FROM_HASH):
        lines.append(f"    - {item}")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "  truth_locks:",
            "    runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "    implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "    deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "    final_commit_as_v1_4_0: NOT_REQUESTED",
            "  files:",
        ]
    )

    for digest, relative in rows:
        lines.extend(
            [
                f"    - path: {relative}",
                f"      sha256: {digest}",
            ]
        )

    write_text(CHECKSUM_MANIFEST, "\n".join(lines))

def write_report(rows: list[tuple[str, str]], verification_failures: list[str]) -> None:
    overall = "PASS" if not verification_failures else "FAIL"

    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS v1.4.0-rc1 Checksum Report",
        "",
        f"Generated at: {now_iso()}",
        f"Package root: {ROOT}",
        f"Overall status: {overall}",
        f"Files hashed: {len(rows)}",
        "",
        "## Preconditions",
        "",
        "- package root exists: PASS",
        "- parseability report passed: PASS",
        "- truth locks preserved: PASS",
        "",
        "## Outputs",
        "",
        f"- {CHECKSUM_FILE.as_posix()}",
        f"- {CHECKSUM_MANIFEST.as_posix()}",
        "",
        "## Verification",
        "",
    ]

    if verification_failures:
        for failure in verification_failures:
            lines.append(f"- FAIL: {failure}")
    else:
        lines.append("- PASS: all generated checksums verified")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "## Locked boundaries",
            "",
            "- runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "- implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "- deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "- final_commit_as_v1_4_0: NOT_REQUESTED",
            "",
            "## Next command",
            "",
            "    /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-ZIP-EXPORT" if overall == "PASS" else "    Fix checksum failures, then rerun checksums.",
        ]
    )

    write_text(CHECKSUM_REPORT, "\n".join(lines))

def main() -> int:
    try:
        assert_package_exists()
        assert_parseability_passed()
        assert_truth_locks()

        files = collect_files()
        rows = write_checksums(files)
        verification_failures = verify_checksums(rows)

        write_manifest(rows)
        write_report(rows, verification_failures)

        if verification_failures:
            print("Overall: FAIL")
            print(f"Failures: {len(verification_failures)}")
            print(f"Report written: {CHECKSUM_REPORT.resolve()}")
            return 1

        update_checkpoint()

        print("Overall: PASS")
        print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
        print(f"Files hashed: {len(rows)}")
        print(f"Checksums written: {CHECKSUM_FILE.resolve()}")
        print(f"Checksum manifest written: {CHECKSUM_MANIFEST.resolve()}")
        print(f"Report written: {CHECKSUM_REPORT.resolve()}")
        print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, deployment_truth=BLOCKED")
        print("Final commit as v1.4.0: NOT_REQUESTED")
````

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import sys
import zipfile

ROOT = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1")

ZIP_PATH = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1_CANDIDATE.zip")
ZIP_SHA_PATH = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1_CANDIDATE.zip.sha256")
ZIP_REPORT_PATH = Path("PROMETHEUS_UNIFIED_PORTABLE_PACKET_v1.4.0-rc1_ZIP_EXPORT_REPORT.md")

CHECKSUM_FILE = ROOT / "release" / "checksums.sha256"
CHECKSUM_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "checksum_report.md"
PARSEABILITY_REPORT = ROOT / "reports" / "parseability_report.md"

def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content.strip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

def relative_posix(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def assert_package_exists() -> None:
    if not ROOT.exists() or not ROOT.is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Package root missing. Run the materializer first.")

def assert_prior_gates_passed() -> None:
    if not PARSEABILITY_REPORT.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("Parseability report missing.")

    if "Overall status: PASS" not in read_text(PARSEABILITY_REPORT):
        raise RuntimeError("Parseability gate has not passed.")

    if not CHECKSUM_REPORT.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("Checksum report missing.")

    if "Overall status: PASS" not in read_text(CHECKSUM_REPORT):
        raise RuntimeError("Checksum gate has not passed.")

    if not CHECKSUM_FILE.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("checksums.sha256 missing.")

def assert_truth_locks() -> None:
    required = {
        "manifest.yaml": [
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED",
        ],
        "core/checkpoint_lock.yaml": [
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
            "v1_4_0_rc1_final_commit: NOT_REQUESTED",
        ],
        "release/candidate_export_record.yaml": [
            "final_commit: false",
            "runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
            "implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
            "deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        ],
    }

    failures: list[str] = []

    for relative, terms in required.items():
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            failures.append(f"{relative}: missing")
            continue

        content = read_text(path)

        for term in terms:
            if term not in content:
                failures.append(f"{relative}: missing lock {term}")

    if failures:
        raise RuntimeError("Truth lock validation failed: " + "; ".join(failures))

def parse_checksum_file() -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    rows: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    for line in read_text(CHECKSUM_FILE).splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()

        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("PENDING"):
            continue

        parts = stripped.split(maxsplit=1)

        if len(parts) != 2:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid checksum line: {line}")

        digest, relative = parts
        rows.append((digest, relative.strip()))

    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("No checksum rows found.")

    return rows

def verify_existing_checksums(rows: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
    failures: list[str] = []

    for expected_digest, relative in rows:
        path = ROOT / relative

        if not path.exists():
            failures.append(f"{relative}: missing")
            continue

        actual_digest = sha256_file(path)

        if actual_digest != expected_digest:
            failures.append(f"{relative}: checksum mismatch")

    if failures:
        raise RuntimeError("Existing checksum verification failed: " + "; ".join(failures))

def collect_package_files() -> list[Path]:
    files = [path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if path.is_file()]
    return sorted(files, key=lambda item: item.as_posix())

def create_zip(files: list[Path]) -> None:
    if ZIP_PATH.exists():
        ZIP_PATH.unlink()

    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, mode="w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        for path in files:
            archive.write(path, arcname=f"{ROOT.name}/{relative_posix(path)}")

def verify_zip() -> int:
    if not ZIP_PATH.exists() or not ZIP_PATH.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError("ZIP was not created.")

    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_PATH, mode="r") as archive:
        bad_file = archive.testzip()

        if bad_file is not None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"ZIP integrity test failed at: {bad_file}")

        return len(archive.namelist())

def write_zip_sha() -> str:
    digest = sha256_file(ZIP_PATH)
    write_text(ZIP_SHA_PATH, f"{digest}  {ZIP_PATH.name}")
    return digest

def write_report(file_count: int, zip_entry_count: int, zip_digest: str) -> None:
    lines = [
        "# PROMETHEUS v1.4.0-rc1 ZIP Export Report",
        "",
        f"Generated at: {now_iso()}",
        "Overall status: PASS",
        f"Package root: {ROOT}",
        f"ZIP: {ZIP_PATH}",
        f"ZIP sha256 file: {ZIP_SHA_PATH}",
        f"Package files included: {file_count}",
        f"ZIP entries: {zip_entry_count}",
        f"ZIP sha256: {zip_digest}",
        "",
        "## Preconditions",
        "",
        "- package root exists: PASS",
        "- parseability passed: PASS",
        "- checksums passed: PASS",
        "- existing checksums verified before ZIP: PASS",
        "- truth locks preserved: PASS",
        "",
        "## Locked boundaries",
        "",
        "- runtime_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- implementation_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- deployment_truth: BLOCKED",
        "- final_commit_as_v1_4_0: NOT_REQUESTED",
        "",
        "## Next command",
        "",
        "    /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-FINAL-REVIEW",
    ]

    write_text(ZIP_REPORT_PATH, "\n".join(lines))

def main() -> int:
    try:
        assert_package_exists()
        assert_prior_gates_passed()
        assert_truth_locks()

        checksum_rows = parse_checksum_file()
        verify_existing_checksums(checksum_rows)

        package_files = collect_package_files()
        create_zip(package_files)

        zip_entry_count = verify_zip()
        zip_digest = write_zip_sha()

        write_report(
            file_count=len(package_files),
            zip_entry_count=zip_entry_count,
            zip_digest=zip_digest,
        )

        print("Overall: PASS")
        print(f"Package root: {ROOT.resolve()}")
        print(f"ZIP created: {ZIP_PATH.resolve()}")
        print(f"ZIP sha256: {ZIP_SHA_PATH.resolve()}")
        print(f"ZIP export report: {ZIP_REPORT_PATH.resolve()}")
        print(f"Package files included: {len(package_files)}")
        print(f"ZIP entries: {zip_entry_count}")
        print("Truth locks: runtime_truth=BLOCKED, implementation_truth=BLOCKED, deployment_truth=BLOCKED")
        print("Final commit as v1.4.0: NOT_REQUESTED")
        print("Next command: /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-FINAL-REVIEW")
        return 0

    except Exception as exc:
        print("Overall: FAIL")
        print(f"Reason: {exc}")
        print("Next action: fix the reported issue, then rerun /PROMETHEUS-v1.4.0-rc1-ZIP-EXPORT")
        return 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
````

> **Proponiendo un paquete para un proyecto**
>

Pregunto por el nombre del proyecto o detalles para personalizar. Prometheus está disponible, y no parece necesitar archivos locales de más.
>
> **Creando un paquete para el proyecto**
>
> La usuaria pregunta si podemos hacer un paquete. La respuesta es sí, se puede en dos modos: un paquete ligero para chat inmediato o un paquete físico con carpetas y zip si tenemos contenido del proyecto. Si se trata de un proyecto específico, como Prometheus, mencionamos eso, pero si no, pedimos nombre y objetivo sin hacer preguntas adicionales.
>
> Pensado durante 16s

Sí. **Ya podemos hacer un packet para un proyecto aquí mismo**, usando PROMETHEUS como estructura de preservación y METANO_IA como sistema madre.

Lo haríamos así:

```yaml id="project-packet-capability"
PROJECT_PACKET:
  can_build_here: true
  modes:
    1_chat_packet:
      output: packet documental en la conversación
      includes:
        - project_identity
        - source_registry
        - decision_log
        - verification_matrix
        - checkpoint_lock
        - command_palette
        - rehydration_card
        - next_action_menu

    2_physical_packet:
      output: carpeta + ZIP descargable
      includes:
        - README.md
        - manifest.yaml
        - source_registry.yaml
        - decision_log.yaml
        - checkpoint_lock.yaml
        - rehydration_card.md
        - release_notes.md
        - checksums.sha256
        - zip.sha256

  truth_policy:
    runtime_truth: BLOCKED unless source-backed
    implementation_truth: BLOCKED unless source-backed
    deployment_truth: BLOCKED unless source-backed
```

# Lo mínimo que necesito para iniciar

```yaml id="packet-intake-minimum"
project_packet_intake:
  project_name: ""
  project_type: ""
  purpose: ""
  current_sources:
    - chat_context
    - uploaded_files
    - notes_or_brief
  intended_output:
    - rehydration_packet
    - handoff_packet
    - working_packet
    - archive_packet
    - production_packet_candidate
  strict_boundaries:
    - what_is_known
    - what_is_missing
    - what_must_not_be_invented
```

# Comando recomendado

Para arrancarlo limpio:

```text id="next-command"
/PROJECT-PACKET-SEED
```

Y lo ejecutaría con este formato:

```yaml id="project-packet-seed-template"
PROJECT_PACKET_SEED:
  project_name: "[nombre_del_proyecto]"
  parent_system: METANO_IA
  governance_subsystem: PROMETHEUS
  status: SEED_PACKET_DRAFT

  packet_scope:
    - documentation
    - workflow
    - source_registry
    - decision_tracking
    - rehydration
    - handoff

  blocked_by_default:
    - runtime_truth
    - implementation_truth
    - deployment_truth
    - final_commit
```

## 1. Núcleo del proyecto

**A.Editorial Emergente** opera como una rama editorial-productiva dentro del ecosistema **Iridiscenter**, usando como gobierno interno el metasistema:

```text
BOS → OPAL → LUMINA → ÆQUITAS → Producción
```

El mapa del sistema ubica a **BOS** como núcleo metaanalítico, **OPAL** como capa simbólica/narrativa, **LUMINA** como percepción estética y producción editorial, y **ÆQUITAS** como auditoría ética, filosófica y de coherencia. 

El proyecto no es solo “hacer diseños” o “hacer documentos”: es construir un **sistema editorial visual verificable**, capaz de producir cuadernillos, guías, galerías, brand boards, prompts, paquetes ZIP, manifests, QA reports y materiales listos para Canva/HTML/DOCX/PDF.

R03_A ya fue incorporada como rama editorial productiva, no solo como referencia. Su función es articular arquitectura de marca derivada de `gem-tokens-v4`, voz autoral LUMINA y despliegue transmedia BOS.

## 3. Dos líneas productivas principales

### A. Línea visual-simbólica: NIDUS_GEM

Esta línea produce el sistema visual de **ABBA + GEM01..GEM12**.

Su estructura actual:

```yaml
NIDUS_GEM_ABBA_STACK: LOCKED_ACTIVE
inheritance_model: PARENT_REFERENCE_ANALOGOUS
template_lock: ACTIVE
prompt_logic: ACTIVE
museum_hydrate_snapshot: ACTIVE
raw_log_preservation: ACTIVE
next_node_ready: GEM-5
canon_lock: false
```

ABBA es el **modelo padre**: no se copia literalmente, sino que entrega gramática visual, estructura editorial y linaje simbólico. Cada GEM hereda estructura y cambia identidad, color, atmósfera, símbolo, arquetipo y referencias. 

Fórmula operativa:

```text
ABBA structural grammar
+ GEM node identity
+ approved references
+ artifact type
+ lock rules
= production-ready visual output
```

La regla central: **ABBA da esqueleto; GEM da personalidad.** 

### B. Línea editorial-formativa: BOS / LUMINA Editorial

Esta línea produce cuadernillos, guías, materiales imprimibles, HTML interactivo y futuros productos editoriales.

## 4. Producto editorial base: Cuadernillos Reflexivos

Los cuadernillos son definidos como **espacios de reflexión encarnada**: no libros que solo se leen, sino campos donde la persona escribe lo que es. Sus líneas actuales son:

```text
LITERATURA → textos sagrados ↔ vida interior
CLÍNICA → procesos emocionales ↔ autoconocimiento
ESPIRITUAL → vocación ↔ transformación
```

Su estructura técnica:

```yaml
total_entries: 20
secciones:
  - Apertura: entries 1-4
  - Exploración: entries 5-16
  - Síntesis: entries 17-18
  - Cierre: entries 19-20
formatos:
  - markdown
  - docx
  - pdf
  - html
  - json
  - txt
```

La especificación confirma que los cuadernillos son instancias de directrices LUMINA, auditables por ÆQUITAS y con trazabilidad registrada en JSON.

## 5. Sistema visual y UI actual

El sistema visual NIDUS tiene una galería activa:

```yaml
build: NIDUS_GEM_VISUAL_GALLERY_v1_ALL
primary_mode: VISUAL_PRODUCTION_FIRST

```

La galería debe mantenerse como **infraestructura de curaduría**, no como documento canónico final. Su objetivo es organizar assets, referencias, compilaciones, versiones, errores útiles, descartes y estados de revisión. 

Reglas duras del sistema visual:

```text
No construir canon final.
No confiar en texto generado dentro de imágenes AI.
gem-tokens-v4 sobrescribe etiquetas generadas.
Preservar paths, provenance, clasificación y metadata.
Mantener warnings visibles.
No convertir la galería en dashboard genérico.
No usar estética dark-tech, neón duro o clutter.
Si hay duda: UNCLASSIFIED_PENDING_REVIEW.
```

## 7. Reglas de verdad y canon

Este proyecto distingue entre:

```text
SOURCE_TRUTH → datos canónicos validados
WORKING_REFERENCE → referencia útil
PREFERRED → referencia fuerte
TEMPLATE_MASTER → estructura reutilizable
USEFUL_ERROR → error que enseña dirección
DISCARD_OR_REVIEW → pendiente o descartable
CANON_LOCKED → solo tras validación explícita
```

Regla crítica:

```text
El texto dentro de imágenes generadas por AI no es canon.
El canon sale de gem-tokens-v4, registry, documentos bloqueados y overrides validados.
```

---

La voz editorial del sistema es:

```text
Contemplativa
Rigurosa
```


---

## 9. Pipeline operativo

El pipeline editorial declarado es:

```text
Corpus fuente
→ aplicar estilo A1
→ usar template maestro
→ instanciar línea / nodo
→ convertir MD → DOCX → PDF / HTML / JSON
→ auditar con ÆQUITAS
→ empaquetar ZIP
```

La auditoría ÆQUITAS evalúa:

```text
Fidelity: fidelidad a voz LUMINA
Coherence: consistencia de paleta, tipo y estructura
Necessity: función real de cada elemento
Precision: preguntas abiertas pero específicas
Elegance: respiración visual, sin saturación
```

---

# 3. PROMETHEUS packet structure

```yaml id="prometheus-packet-structure"
PROMETHEUS_PACKET:
  00_project_identity:
    purpose: "Preservar, rehidratar y producir artefactos editoriales/visuales NIDUS con trazabilidad."
    parent_system: METANO_IA
    governed_by: PROMETHEUS
    operational_branch: R03_A · Editorial Emergente

  01_source_registry:
    include:
      - source_id
      - filename
      - source_class
      - authority_level
      - canon_status
      - cited_evidence
      - missing_fields
      - sha256_if_available

  02_decision_log:
    include:
      - decision_id
      - decision_text
      - source_backing
      - accepted_scope
      - blocked_scope
      - date_or_sequence
      - next_dependency

  03_verification_matrix:
    gates:
      - source_backed
      - no_generated_text_as_canon
      - no_final_lock_without_AEQUITAS
      - preserve_provenance
      - metadata_export_ready
      - package_manifest_ready

  04_checkpoint_lock:
    status: DRAFT_ONLY
    lockable_when:
      - source registry complete
      - decision log reconciled
      - missing sources declared
      - ÆQUITAS pass issued
      - package checksum generated

  05_command_palette:
    commands:
      - /PROJECT-PACKET-SEED
      - /PROJECT-SOURCE-REGISTRY
      - /PROJECT-DECISION-LOG
      - /PROJECT-VERIFICATION-MATRIX
      - /PROJECT-CHECKPOINT-LOCK
      - /PROJECT-REHYDRATION-CARD
      - /PROJECT-PACKET-EXPORT

  06_rehydration_card:
    output: compact context block for new chat / handoff / agent

  07_next_action_menu:
    options:
      - build chat packet
      - build physical ZIP packet
      - normalize source registry first
      - freeze checkpoint draft
      - continue GEM05 production
```
